考虑一个 OFDM 系统的设计：目标是在 600 kHz 的可用带宽内，用 16-QAM 以 2 Mbps 的速率传输数据。已知信道时延扩展最大为 20 μs，可设循环前缀等于最大时延扩展。求子载波总数和 OFDM 符号周期。

Δf = B/N循环前缀开销讲义第 7 章 p.8、p.17–18
题意讲解：这道题到底要干嘛

场景题目在讲一件什么事

设计题：要用 OFDM 在 600 kHz 带宽里传 2 Mbps，调制用 16-QAM。信道时延扩展最多 20 µs，循环前缀（CP）就取 20 µs。

求子载波数 N 和 OFDM 符号时长。

符号题目里每个量是什么

16-QAM每个符号 4 bit
N子载波数：一个 OFDM 符号里同时发 N 个 QAM 符号
Δf = B/N子载波间隔；有用符号时长 T = 1/Δf = N/B
CP循环前缀 20 µs，每个 OFDM 符号前面都要加，占时间但不带新数据

要求每一问在问什么

  • 唯一一问 列出"速率 = 每个 OFDM 符号的比特数 ÷ OFDM 符号总时长"：4N /(N/B + 20 µs) = 2 Mbps，解出 N，再算 OFDM 符号时长（含 CP）。

注意读题容易误会的地方

  • OFDM 符号总时长 = 有用部分 N/B + CP，别漏 CP。

目的这道题想让你明白什么

没有 CP 的话，600 kHz 本来就够用，N 取多少都行；正是 CP 的开销决定了 N：N 越大，CP 占的比例越小。

一句话：由"4 bit × N 个子载波 ÷（N/B + 20 µs）= 2 Mbps"解出子载波数 N，再算含 CP 的 OFDM 符号时长。
  1. 16-QAM 每符号 4 bit：需要的总符号速率 2 Mbps / 4 = 500 k 符号/秒。
  2. N 个子载波：有用符号时长 T = N/B = N/600k，加 CP 后一个 OFDM 符号长 T + 20 µs，期间 N 个子载波各发一个符号。
  3. \(\dfrac{N}{N/600\text{k}+20\,\mu s}=500\text{k}\) → \(N=0.8333N+10\) → N = 60。
  4. T = 60/600 kHz = 100 µs；OFDM 符号总长 100 + 20 = 120 µs = 0.12 ms。
N = 60，OFDM 符号时长 0.12 ms
官方答案：60；0.12 ms ✓
如果不加 CP，600 kHz 本来就够发 500 k 符号/秒，N 取多少都行，题目就没有唯一解。正是 CP 的开销决定了 N：N 越大，CP 占的比例越小。CP 占 20/120 = 1/6 的时间，所以带宽必须比 500 k 多出 1/5，正好是 600 kHz。
知识讲解 + 详细解析：由速率和 CP 解出子载波数

知识讲解OFDM 的时间账：有用部分 + 循环前缀

OFDM 把带宽 B 分成 N 个子载波，子载波间隔 Δf = B/N（第 7 章 p.8）。为了让子载波相互正交，有用符号时长必须是 T = 1/Δf = N/B（p.7）。在这段时间里，N 个子载波各发一个调制符号。

每个 OFDM 块前面还要加一段保护间隔 TG（实现上就是循环前缀 CP），TG ≥ 最大时延扩展 TD，用来挡住上一个块的回波（p.5、p.17）。所以一个 OFDM 块总长 T + TG（p.18），但 CP 不带新数据。

数据速率 = N × log₂M bit ÷ (N/B + TG)

符号：M 是调制阶数，16-QAM 的 log₂16 = 4 bit/符号；B = 600 kHz；TG = 20 µs。CP 占用时间还带来功率损失 10 log₁₀((T + TG)/T) dB（p.18）。

详细解析四步解出 N 和符号时长

  1. 比特速率换成符号速率。为什么：一个子载波一次发一个 QAM 符号，按符号数列方程更方便。2 Mbps ÷ 4 bit = 500 k 符号/秒。
  2. 列方程。每个 OFDM 块发 N 个符号，耗时 N/B + TG：
    N / (N/600 kHz + 20 µs) = 500 k 符号/秒
  3. 解方程。两边乘以分母：N = 500k × N/600k + 500k × 20 × 10⁻⁶ = 0.8333N + 10 → 0.1667N = 10 → N = 60。
  4. 算时长。T = N/B = 60/600 kHz = 100 µs（Δf = 10 kHz）；OFDM 符号总长 T + TG = 100 + 20 = 120 µs = 0.12 ms。

更快的想法：不加 CP 时 600 kHz 能发 600 k 符号/秒，只需要 500 k，所以有用时间只能占 500/600 = 5/6，CP 占 1/6：TG = T/5 → T = 5 × 20 = 100 µs → N = B·T = 600 k × 100 µs = 60。

自检回代检查

  • 速率：60 × 4 bit / 120 µs = 2 × 10⁶ bit/s = 2 Mbps ✓。
  • 每个子载波：每 120 µs 发一个符号，8.33 k 符号/秒，× 60 = 500 k 符号/秒 ✓。
  • 平坦吗：相干带宽约 BC ≈ 1/TD = 1/20 µs = 50 kHz（第 7 章 p.14 用 TD = 1/Bc），子载波宽 10 kHz < 50 kHz ✓。
  • CP 功率损失：10 log₁₀(120/100) = 0.79 dB。

易错常见丢分点

  • 忘了 CP：没有 CP 时 600 kHz 本来就够发 500 k 符号/秒，N 取多少都行，题目就没有唯一解。正是 CP 的开销决定了 N。
  • 直接拿 2 Mbps 列方程，忘了先除以 4 bit/符号。
  • 单位：N/600 kHz 的单位是秒，60/600 000 = 10⁻⁴ s = 100 µs；20 µs = 2 × 10⁻⁵ s。
  • "OFDM symbol duration"官方答案 0.12 ms 是含 CP 的总长；如果题目问有用符号时长，答 100 µs。两个都写出来最稳。

考试英文写法

16-QAM carries log₂16 = 4 bits/symbol, so the required symbol rate is 2 Mbps / 4 = 500 k symbols/s.

With N sub-carriers, the useful OFDM symbol duration is T = N/B and each OFDM block lasts T + TG with TG = 20 µs, carrying N symbols: \(\dfrac{N}{N/(600\text{ kHz})+20\,\mu\text{s}}=500\text{ k}\Rightarrow N=0.8333N+10\Rightarrow N=60\).

Useful duration T = 60/600 kHz = 100 µs (Δf = 10 kHz); OFDM symbol duration including the CP = 100 + 20 = 120 µs = 0.12 ms. Check: 60 × 4 bits / 120 µs = 2 Mbps.

一句话：2 Mbps ÷ 4 = 500 k 符号/秒；N/(N/600 kHz + 20 µs) = 500 k → N = 60；T = 100 µs，加 CP 后 OFDM 符号长 120 µs = 0.12 ms。
◆ 本题知识点
考什么
OFDM 系统设计：子载波数、OFDM 符号时长、CP 开销（第 7 章 p.8、p.17–18）。
前置知识
有用符号时长 T = N/B = 1/Δf；一个 OFDM 块 = T + TG，期间每个子载波发一个符号。
解题套路
① 比特速率 ÷ log₂M = 需要的符号速率；② 列方程 N / (N/B + TG) = 符号速率；③ 解 N；④ T = N/B，总时长 T + TG。
关键数字
2 Mbps / 4 = 500 k 符号/秒；N = 0.8333N + 10 → N = 60；T = 100 µs，总长 120 µs。
检验
60 × 4 bit / 120 µs = 2 Mbps；CP 占 1/6 的时间，功率损失 10 log(120/100) = 0.79 dB。
举一反三
时延扩展变成 40 µs：N = 0.8333N + 20 → N = 120，T = 200 µs，总长 240 µs。时延扩展越大，需要的 N 越大，CP 的占比才不至于太高。
易错
没有 CP 时 N 取多少都行，题目就没有唯一解，所以一定要把 CP 算进时长；速率是比特速率，要先除以 4。
  1. 16-QAM carries 4 bits per symbol: the required total symbol rate is 2 Mbps / 4 = 500 k symbols/s.
  2. With N sub-carriers: useful symbol duration T = N/B = N/600k; with the CP one OFDM symbol lasts T + 20 µs, during which each of the N sub-carriers sends one symbol.
  3. \(\dfrac{N}{N/600\text{k}+20\,\mu s}=500\text{k}\) → \(N=0.8333N+10\) → N = 60.
  4. T = 60/600 kHz = 100 µs; total OFDM symbol 100 + 20 = 120 µs = 0.12 ms.
N = 60, OFDM symbol duration 0.12 ms
Official answer: 60; 0.12 ms ✓
Without the CP, 600 kHz would carry 500 k symbols/s for any N and the problem would have no unique answer. The CP overhead is what fixes N: the larger N, the smaller the CP fraction. Here the CP takes 20/120 = 1/6 of the time, so the bandwidth must exceed 500 k by 1/5, exactly 600 kHz.
Concept + walkthrough: N from rate and CP

ConceptOFDM time budget: useful part + cyclic prefix

OFDM divides the bandwidth B into N sub-carriers with spacing Δf = B/N (Ch 7 p.8). For the sub-carriers to be orthogonal, the useful symbol duration must be T = 1/Δf = N/B (p.7). During this time each of the N sub-carriers sends one modulated symbol.

Each OFDM block is also preceded by a guard interval TG (implemented as a cyclic prefix, CP), with TG ≥ the maximum delay spread TD, to block the echo of the previous block (p.5, p.17). So one OFDM block lasts T + TG in total (p.18), but the CP carries no new data.

Data rate = N × log₂M bit ÷ (N/B + TG)

Symbols: M is the modulation order, log₂16 = 4 bits/symbol for 16-QAM; B = 600 kHz; TG = 20 µs. The time spent on the CP also costs power: 10 log₁₀((T + TG)/T) dB (p.18).

Step by stepFour steps to N and the symbol duration

  1. Bit rate to symbol rate. Why: each sub-carrier sends one QAM symbol at a time, so it is easier to count symbols. 2 Mbps ÷ 4 bits = 500 k symbols/s.
  2. Set up the equation. Each OFDM block carries N symbols and takes N/B + TG:
    N / (N/600 kHz + 20 µs) = 500 k symbols/s
  3. Solve. Multiply both sides by the denominator: N = 500k × N/600k + 500k × 20 × 10⁻⁶ = 0.8333N + 10 → 0.1667N = 10 → N = 60.
  4. Durations. T = N/B = 60/600 kHz = 100 µs (Δf = 10 kHz); total OFDM symbol T + TG = 100 + 20 = 120 µs = 0.12 ms.

A quicker way: without a CP, 600 kHz could carry 600 k symbols/s, but only 500 k are needed, so the useful time can take only 500/600 = 5/6 of each block and the CP takes 1/6: TG = T/5 → T = 5 × 20 = 100 µs → N = B·T = 600 k × 100 µs = 60.

CheckSubstitute back

  • Rate: 60 × 4 bits / 120 µs = 2 × 10⁶ bit/s = 2 Mbps ✓.
  • Per sub-carrier: one symbol every 120 µs, 8.33 k symbols/s, × 60 = 500 k symbols/s ✓.
  • Flat? The coherence bandwidth is about BC ≈ 1/TD = 1/20 µs = 50 kHz (Ch 7 p.14 uses TD = 1/Bc); the sub-carrier width 10 kHz < 50 kHz ✓.
  • CP power loss: 10 log₁₀(120/100) = 0.79 dB.

PitfallsWhere marks are lost

  • Forgetting the CP: without it, 600 kHz is already enough for 500 k symbols/s, any N works and there is no unique answer. It is the CP overhead that fixes N.
  • Using 2 Mbps directly in the equation without first dividing by 4 bits/symbol.
  • Units: N/600 kHz is in seconds, 60/600 000 = 10⁻⁴ s = 100 µs; 20 µs = 2 × 10⁻⁵ s.
  • The official "OFDM symbol duration" of 0.12 ms is the total including the CP; if a question asks for the useful symbol duration, answer 100 µs. Writing both is safest.

ExamHow to write it

16-QAM carries log₂16 = 4 bits/symbol, so the required symbol rate is 2 Mbps / 4 = 500 k symbols/s.

With N sub-carriers, the useful OFDM symbol duration is T = N/B and each OFDM block lasts T + TG with TG = 20 µs, carrying N symbols: \(\dfrac{N}{N/(600\text{ kHz})+20\,\mu\text{s}}=500\text{ k}\Rightarrow N=0.8333N+10\Rightarrow N=60\).

Useful duration T = 60/600 kHz = 100 µs (Δf = 10 kHz); OFDM symbol duration including the CP = 100 + 20 = 120 µs = 0.12 ms. Check: 60 × 4 bits / 120 µs = 2 Mbps.

In one line: 2 Mbps ÷ 4 = 500 k symbols/s; N/(N/600 kHz + 20 µs) = 500 k → N = 60; T = 100 µs, and with the CP the OFDM symbol lasts 120 µs = 0.12 ms.
◆ Key points
What it tests
OFDM system design: number of sub-carriers, OFDM symbol duration, CP overhead (Ch 7 p.8, p.17–18).
Background
Useful symbol duration T = N/B = 1/Δf; one OFDM block lasts T + TG, during which each sub-carrier sends one symbol.
Method
① Bit rate ÷ log₂M = required symbol rate; ② write N / (N/B + TG) = symbol rate; ③ solve for N; ④ T = N/B, total T + TG.
Key numbers
2 Mbps / 4 = 500 k symbols/s; N = 0.8333N + 10 → N = 60; T = 100 µs, total 120 µs.
Check
60 × 4 bit / 120 µs = 2 Mbps; the CP takes 1/6 of the time, power loss 10 log(120/100) = 0.79 dB.
Variations
With a 40 µs delay spread: N = 0.8333N + 20 → N = 120, T = 200 µs, total 240 µs. A larger delay spread needs a larger N to keep the CP fraction small.
Common mistakes
Without a CP any N would do and there would be no unique answer, so the CP must be included in the duration; the given rate is a bit rate, divide by 4 first.
P7 · Q3

最短的循环前缀

Original question · 中英对照Practice 7

The Z–transform of a mobile channel is \(H(Z)=1+0.9Z^{-1}\). In order to mitigate the ISI of this channel, the system is now designed with OFDM. What is the minimum cyclic-prefix required. Explain your answer.

某移动信道的 Z 变换为 \(H(Z)=1+0.9Z^{-1}\)。为抑制该信道的 ISI，系统改用 OFDM 设计。所需的最小循环前缀是多少？说明理由。

信道长度CP ≥ L − 1讲义第 7 章 p.17–22
题意讲解：这道题到底要干嘛

场景题目在讲一件什么事

信道 H(z) = 1 + 0.9z⁻¹：一条直射，加一条晚一个采样到达、强度 0.9 的回波。

系统用 OFDM 来对付这个回波造成的 ISI，问循环前缀（CP）至少要多长。

符号题目里每个量是什么

\(z^{-1}\)延迟一个采样
L信道长度：冲激响应 [1, 0.9] 有 2 个抽头，L = 2
CP循环前缀：把每个 OFDM 块尾部的几个采样复制到块前面

要求每一问在问什么

  • 唯一一问 求最短 CP 并解释：上一个块会漏进下一个块几个采样？CP 要盖住它们，接收端把 CP 丢掉，ISI 就跟着丢掉了；用块尾的复制品还能让子载波保持正交。

注意读题容易误会的地方

  • 是 L − 1 = 1 个采样，不是 L = 2 个；系数 0.9 的大小不影响 CP 长度，只看延迟几个采样。

目的这道题想让你明白什么

CP 的长度只取决于信道"记忆"有多长（最大时延），至少等于 L − 1 个采样。

一句话：信道有一个晚 1 个采样的回波，问 OFDM 的循环前缀至少多长才能挡住 ISI，并解释原因。
  1. 冲激响应 h = [1, 0.9]：一条直射，加一条晚一个采样到达的回波，信道长度 L = 2，最大时延扩展 = 1 个采样。
  2. 上一个 OFDM 块的最后一个采样，会通过 0.9z⁻¹ 漏进下一个块的第一个采样。CP 至少要盖住这 1 个采样，才能把 ISI 挡在 CP 里（接收端丢掉 CP）。
  3. CP 用块尾的复制品，线性卷积在接收窗口里等价于循环卷积，DFT 后正好得到 \(H_ks_k\)，子载波保持正交。
最短 CP = 1 个采样（符号）长度 = L − 1
官方答案：1 symbol length ✓
知识讲解 + 详细解析：最短循环前缀 = L − 1

知识讲解信道"记忆"和循环前缀

从 H(z) 读冲激响应：z⁻¹ 表示晚一个采样，H(z) = 1 + 0.9z⁻¹ → h = [1, 0.9]，一条直射 + 一条晚 1 个采样、强度 0.9 的回波。抽头数 L = 2，信道的"记忆"（最大时延）是 L − 1 = 1 个采样：每个输出样本 ri = xi + 0.9xi−1，会用到前一个输入样本。

问题在块边界：OFDM 一块一块地发（每块是 N 个子载波符号做 IDFT 得到的 N 个采样，第 7 章 p.19）。一个块的第一个采样 r₀ = x₀ + 0.9 × (上一块的最后一个采样)，混进了上一块的数据，这就是块间的 ISI。

循环前缀 CP（p.21–22）：把每块最后 G 个采样复制一份放到块前面，接收端把这 G 个采样丢掉，只留后 N 个。G ≥ L − 1 时，上一块的回波只会落在被丢掉的 CP 里。

为什么要"复制块尾"而不是放空白：放 CP 后，块的第一个采样前面"看到"的是本块的最后一个采样，线性卷积在接收窗口里就变成循环卷积。循环卷积做 DFT 正好变成乘法：Rk = HkSk + Wk（p.17、p.22），每个子载波只乘一个复数，子载波之间保持正交。如果只放空白（补零），能挡住 ISI，但接收窗口里不再是循环结构，子载波失去正交，产生载波间干扰（ICI）。

详细解析用 N = 4 的小例子看清楚

  1. 发送：一块的采样是 x₀ x₁ x₂ x₃，加 1 个采样的 CP 后发 [x₃ | x₀ x₁ x₂ x₃]。
  2. 接收（ri = xi + 0.9xi−1）：
    CP 位置：x₃ + 0.9 × (上一块最后一个采样) ← 有 ISI，但会被丢掉
    r₀ = x₀ + 0.9x₃（前一个采样正好是 CP 里的 x₃）
    r₁ = x₁ + 0.9x₀，r₂ = x₂ + 0.9x₁，r₃ = x₃ + 0.9x₂
  3. 看结构：留下的 r₀…r₃ 里每个 x 都只和它"循环意义下的前一个"相加（x₀ 的前一个是 x₃），这就是 h 和 x 的 4 点循环卷积，完全没有上一块的数据。
  4. DFT 后：Rk = HkSk，Hk = 1 + 0.9e−j2πk/4 = 1.9、1 − 0.9j、0.1、1 + 0.9j。接收端每个子载波除以 Hk 就完成均衡（p.25–26）。
  5. 结论：最短 CP = L − 1 = 1 个采样（1 个符号长度）。再短（0 个）就挡不住 ISI；更长也可以，但浪费时间，效率 N/(N + G)。

附带一点：H₂ = 0.1，第 2 个子载波几乎被两条路径抵消。OFDM 不能消除衰落，只是把频率选择性衰落变成每个子载波上的平坦衰落，这时就需要编码或 P7 Q1 那样的功率分配。

易错常见丢分点

  • 答成 L = 2 个采样。CP 要盖住的是"回波晚到的那部分"，是 L − 1，不是 L。
  • 以为系数 0.9 越大 CP 要越长。CP 长度只看延迟几个采样，和回波强弱无关。
  • 只说"挡住 ISI"，不说为什么要用块尾的复制品（保持循环卷积 / 子载波正交，避免 ICI）。题目要求 Explain，这一点是得分点。
  • 换成时间单位：CP ≥ 1 个采样间隔 Ts，即 TG ≥ 最大时延扩展 TD。

考试英文写法

From \(H(z)=1+0.9z^{-1}\), the channel impulse response is h = [1, 0.9]: L = 2 taps, so the channel memory (maximum delay spread) is L − 1 = 1 sample.

The last sample of each OFDM block therefore leaks into the first sample of the next block (ISI). A cyclic prefix of G ≥ L − 1 samples, i.e. a copy of the last sample of the block placed in front of it, absorbs this leakage, and the receiver discards the CP.

Because the prefix is cyclic, the linear convolution with h becomes a circular convolution over the N retained samples, so after the DFT \(r_k=H_ks_k+w_k\): no ISI and no ICI, and each sub-carrier is equalised by one complex gain.

Minimum cyclic prefix = 1 symbol (sample) length.

一句话：h = [1, 0.9]，回波晚 1 个采样 → 最短 CP = L − 1 = 1 个符号长度；用块尾复制品做前缀，既把 ISI 留在被丢掉的 CP 里，又让线性卷积变成循环卷积，DFT 后 rk = Hksk + wk。
◆ 本题知识点
考什么
循环前缀的最短长度（第 7 章 p.17–22）。
前置知识
Z 变换：Z⁻¹ 表示晚一个采样；L 个抽头的信道，最长回波晚 L − 1 个采样。
解题套路
由 H(Z) 读出冲激响应 h = [1, 0.9] → L = 2 → CP ≥ L − 1 = 1 个采样。
为什么够
CP ≥ L − 1 时，接收端保留的 N 个采样只含本块的数据，线性卷积变成循环卷积，DFT 后 rk = Hksk + wk（讲解页 7.4 节推导框）。
为什么复制块尾
只放一段空白也能挡住上一块的回声，但接收窗口里看到的不再是完整的周期信号，子载波失去正交，会有 ICI；复制块尾才能保持循环结构。
易错
是 L − 1 = 1 个，不是 L = 2 个；系数 0.9 的大小不影响 CP 长度，只看延迟几个采样。
举一反三
H(Z) = 1 + 0.5Z⁻³ → 延迟 3 个采样 → CP ≥ 3；换成时间就是 TG ≥ 最大时延扩展 TD。CP 每多 1 个采样，效率降为 N/(N + G)。
  1. Impulse response h = [1, 0.9]: a direct path plus an echo one sample later; channel length L = 2, maximum delay spread = 1 sample.
  2. The last sample of the previous OFDM block leaks into the first sample of the next block through 0.9z⁻¹. The CP must cover at least this 1 sample so the ISI stays inside the CP, which the receiver discards.
  3. Because the CP is a copy of the block tail, the linear convolution looks like a circular convolution inside the receive window; after the DFT this gives exactly \(H_ks_k\), and the sub-carriers stay orthogonal.
Minimum CP = 1 sample (symbol) = L − 1
Official answer: 1 symbol length ✓
Concept + walkthrough: minimum CP = L − 1

ConceptChannel "memory" and the cyclic prefix

Read the impulse response from H(z): z⁻¹ means one sample later, so H(z) = 1 + 0.9z⁻¹ → h = [1, 0.9]: a direct path plus an echo 1 sample later with strength 0.9. The number of taps is L = 2 and the channel "memory" (maximum delay) is L − 1 = 1 sample: each output sample ri = xi + 0.9xi−1 uses the previous input sample.

The problem is at block boundaries: OFDM is sent block by block (each block is the N samples obtained by an IDFT of N sub-carrier symbols, Ch 7 p.19). The first sample of a block is r₀ = x₀ + 0.9 × (last sample of the previous block), which mixes in data from the previous block: inter-block ISI.

Cyclic prefix (CP) (p.21–22): copy the last G samples of each block to its front; the receiver discards these G samples and keeps only the last N. With G ≥ L − 1, the echo of the previous block lands only inside the discarded CP.

Why "copy the tail" instead of leaving a gap: with the CP, the sample "seen" before the first sample of the block is the last sample of the same block, so inside the receive window the linear convolution becomes a circular convolution. The DFT turns circular convolution exactly into multiplication: Rk = HkSk + Wk (p.17, p.22); each sub-carrier is multiplied by one complex number and the sub-carriers stay orthogonal. An empty gap (zero padding) also blocks the ISI, but the window no longer has a circular structure, so the sub-carriers lose orthogonality and inter-carrier interference (ICI) appears.

Step by stepA small N = 4 example makes it clear

  1. Transmit: a block has samples x₀ x₁ x₂ x₃; with a 1-sample CP we send [x₃ | x₀ x₁ x₂ x₃].
  2. Receive (ri = xi + 0.9xi−1):
    CP slot: x₃ + 0.9 × (last sample of the previous block) ← contains ISI, but is discarded
    r₀ = x₀ + 0.9x₃ (the previous sample is exactly the x₃ in the CP)
    r₁ = x₁ + 0.9x₀, r₂ = x₂ + 0.9x₁, r₃ = x₃ + 0.9x₂
  3. Look at the structure: in the kept r₀…r₃ each x is added only to its "cyclic predecessor" (the predecessor of x₀ is x₃). This is the 4-point circular convolution of h and x, with no data from the previous block at all.
  4. After the DFT: Rk = HkSk with Hk = 1 + 0.9e−j2πk/4 = 1.9, 1 − 0.9j, 0.1, 1 + 0.9j. The receiver equalises by dividing each sub-carrier by Hk (p.25–26).
  5. Conclusion: minimum CP = L − 1 = 1 sample (1 symbol length). Shorter (0) cannot block the ISI; longer also works but wastes time, efficiency N/(N + G).

A side note: H₂ = 0.1, so sub-carrier 2 is almost cancelled by the two paths. OFDM does not remove fading; it turns frequency-selective fading into flat fading on each sub-carrier, which then needs coding or power allocation as in P7 Q1.

PitfallsWhere marks are lost

  • Answering L = 2 samples. The CP must cover the part of the echo that arrives late, which is L − 1, not L.
  • Thinking a larger coefficient 0.9 needs a longer CP. The CP length depends only on how many samples the echo is delayed, not on its strength.
  • Saying only "it blocks ISI" without saying why the copy of the tail is used (keeps the circular convolution / sub-carrier orthogonality, avoids ICI). The question says "Explain"; this point earns marks.
  • In time units: CP ≥ 1 sampling interval Ts, i.e. TG ≥ the maximum delay spread TD.

ExamHow to write it

From \(H(z)=1+0.9z^{-1}\), the channel impulse response is h = [1, 0.9]: L = 2 taps, so the channel memory (maximum delay spread) is L − 1 = 1 sample.

The last sample of each OFDM block therefore leaks into the first sample of the next block (ISI). A cyclic prefix of G ≥ L − 1 samples, i.e. a copy of the last sample of the block placed in front of it, absorbs this leakage, and the receiver discards the CP.

Because the prefix is cyclic, the linear convolution with h becomes a circular convolution over the N retained samples, so after the DFT \(r_k=H_ks_k+w_k\): no ISI and no ICI, and each sub-carrier is equalised by one complex gain.

Minimum cyclic prefix = 1 symbol (sample) length.

In one line: h = [1, 0.9], the echo is 1 sample late → minimum CP = L − 1 = 1 symbol length; a prefix copied from the block tail keeps the ISI inside the discarded CP and turns the linear convolution into a circular one, so after the DFT rk = Hksk + wk.
◆ Key points
What it tests
The minimum cyclic-prefix length (Ch 7 p.17–22).
Background
Z-transform: Z⁻¹ means one sample later; a channel with L taps has its longest echo L − 1 samples late.
Method
Read the impulse response h = [1, 0.9] from H(Z) → L = 2 → CP ≥ L − 1 = 1 sample.
Why it is enough
With CP ≥ L − 1 the N samples kept by the receiver contain only data from the current block, the linear convolution becomes circular, and after the DFT rk = Hksk + wk (derivation box in Section 7.4 of the learning page).
Why copy the tail
An empty guard interval would also block the previous block's echo, but the receive window would no longer see a complete periodic signal, the sub-carriers would lose orthogonality and ICI would appear. Only copying the tail keeps the circular structure.
Common mistakes
It is L − 1 = 1 sample, not L = 2; the size of the coefficient 0.9 does not matter, only how many samples the echo is delayed.
Variations
H(Z) = 1 + 0.5Z⁻³ → delay 3 samples → CP ≥ 3; in time, TG ≥ the maximum delay spread TD. Each extra CP sample lowers the efficiency to N/(N + G).

模拟 Quiz · 自编 12 题

第 1b 章 + 第 2 章 · 三套（A、B、C）每套 3 题，按 30 分钟练；最后 3 道是专项（自由空间、双径、六种信道效应综合）
模拟 A · Q1

两种信道状态：全概率、贝叶斯与离散 SNR

Original question · 中英对照Mock Quiz A (self-written)

A wireless link alternates between two states. In the "good" state, which occurs with probability 0.7, the bit error probability is \(10^{-4}\). In the "bad" state, which occurs with probability 0.3, the bit error probability is \(10^{-2}\).

一条无线链路在两种状态之间切换。"好"状态出现的概率为 0.7，此时误比特率为 \(10^{-4}\)；"坏"状态出现的概率为 0.3，此时误比特率为 \(10^{-2}\)。

(a) Find the average bit error probability of the link. (3 marks)

(a) 求该链路的平均误比特率。（3 分）

(b) A bit is found to be in error. What is the probability that the link was in the bad state when this bit was sent? (3 marks)

(b) 发现某个比特出错了。发送这个比特时，链路处于"坏"状态的概率是多少？（3 分）

(c) The received SNR is 10 dB in the good state and 0 dB in the bad state. Find the mean and the variance of the SNR (in linear scale), and express the mean SNR in dB. (4 marks)

(c) 好状态下接收 SNR 为 10 dB，坏状态下为 0 dB。求 SNR（线性值）的均值和方差，并把平均 SNR 用 dB 表示。（4 分）

全概率贝叶斯（必背）离散期望与方差讲义第 1b 章 p.5–14
题意讲解：这道题到底要干嘛

场景题目在讲一件什么事

一条无线链路时好时坏：比如有时被遮挡、有时畅通。题目把它简化成两种状态，每种状态出现的概率和误比特率都已知。

这是第 1b 章概率复习的典型题：分情况讨论（全概率）、由结果反推原因（贝叶斯）、离散随机变量的期望和方差。

符号题目里每个量是什么

good / bad两种信道状态，概率分别是 0.7 和 0.3（加起来是 1）
\(10^{-4},\ 10^{-2}\)各状态下的误比特率，也就是条件概率 P(出错 | 状态)
10 dB / 0 dB各状态下的接收 SNR（dB），算均值方差前要换成线性值 10 和 1

要求每一问在问什么

  • (a) 不知道当时处于哪个状态，一个比特出错的总概率是多少：两种情况各自算，再按出现概率加权。
  • (b) 已经知道"这个比特出错了"，反过来问"当时是坏状态"的概率：先有结果、再问原因，就是贝叶斯。
  • (c) 把 SNR 看成一个离散随机变量（10 的概率 0.7，1 的概率 0.3），求它的均值和方差，再把均值换回 dB。

注意读题容易误会的地方

  • (b) 的答案不是 0.3：0.3 是"事先"处于坏状态的概率，题目问的是"知道出错以后"的概率。
  • (c) 不能直接平均 dB 值：dB 是对数，先换成线性值再平均。

目的这道题想让你明白什么

全概率（分情况加权）和贝叶斯（由果推因）是第 1b 章的两个核心工具；还要会求离散分布的期望和方差，并注意 dB 和线性值的换算。

一句话：两状态信道：(a) 用全概率求平均误码率，(b) 用贝叶斯求"出错时处于坏状态"的概率，(c) 求 SNR 的均值和方差（先换线性值）。

思路：(a) 分情况讨论再加权，就是全概率；(b) 已经看到结果（出错），反过来问原因（处于哪个状态），就是贝叶斯；(c) 离散分布求期望和方差，先把 dB 换成线性值。

  1. (a) 全概率：\(P_e=P(G)\,P(e\mid G)+P(B)\,P(e\mid B)=0.7\times10^{-4}+0.3\times10^{-2}=7\times10^{-5}+3\times10^{-3}\)
    P_e = 3.07 × 10⁻³
  2. (b) 贝叶斯：\(P(B\mid e)=\dfrac{P(B)\,P(e\mid B)}{P_e}=\dfrac{3\times10^{-3}}{3.07\times10^{-3}}=0.977\)。分子就是 (a) 里"坏状态"那一项，分母就是 (a) 的答案。
  3. (c) 先换成线性值：10 dB → 10，0 dB → 1。
    E[γ] = 0.7 × 10 + 0.3 × 1 = 7.3　　E[γ²] = 0.7 × 100 + 0.3 × 1 = 70.3
  4. \(\mathrm{Var}(\gamma)=E[\gamma^2]-(E[\gamma])^2=70.3-53.29=17.01\)（标准差 4.12）；平均 SNR \(=10\log_{10}7.3=8.63\) dB。
(a) 3.07 × 10⁻³；(b) 0.977；(c) E[γ] = 7.3（8.63 dB），Var(γ) = 17.0
官方答案：无（自编模拟题）。所有数字已用 src/p2build/verify_mock.py 验算。
直接把 dB 值平均，得到 0.7 × 10 + 0.3 × 0 = 7 dB，是错的：dB 是对数，要先换成线性值再求平均。老师说没做 dB ↔ 线性换算会扣分。另外 (b) 很有代表性：坏状态只占 30% 的时间，却贡献了 97.7% 的错误，误码几乎全来自信道差的时候。
知识讲解 + 详细解析：全概率、贝叶斯与离散期望方差

知识讲解条件概率、全概率、贝叶斯是一套工具

条件概率（conditional probability）P(A | B) 读作"已知 B 发生了，A 发生的概率"。本题给的误比特率就是条件概率：P(e | G) = 10⁻⁴ 的意思是"在好状态下，一个比特出错的概率"。

乘法规则（multiplication rule，讲义第 1b 章 p.6）：P(A ∩ B) = P(A) P(B | A)。例如"处于坏状态并且出错"的概率 = P(B) P(e | B) = 0.3 × 10⁻² = 3 × 10⁻³。

全概率定理（total probability theorem，p.8）：把所有可能的情况 A₁、A₂、……（互不重叠、合起来是全部）分开，每种情况各算"这种情况发生并且 B 发生"，再全部加起来：P(B) = Σ P(Ai) P(B | Ai)。讲义叫它"分而治之"（divide and conquer）。

贝叶斯公式（Bayes' rule，p.9，蓝框必背）：已知结果 B，反过来问原因 Ai：\(P(A_i\mid B)=\dfrac{P(A_i)\,P(B\mid A_i)}{\sum_j P(A_j)\,P(B\mid A_j)}\)。分子是乘法规则，分母就是全概率。P(Ai) 叫先验概率（a priori），P(Ai | B) 叫后验概率（a posteriori）。

老师课上强调：往年有人把 P(B) 只写成一项 P(A₁)P(B | A₁)，全概率必须对所有情况求和；贝叶斯在第 3、6 章（最大似然检测、MLSE）还会反复用到。

把概率换成"个数"最好理解。假设发送 100 万个比特：

状态发送的比特数出错的比特数
好（概率 0.7）70 万70 万 × 10⁻⁴ = 70
坏（概率 0.3）30 万30 万 × 10⁻² = 3000
合计100 万3070

(a) 总误码率 = 3070 / 100 万；(b) 在所有出错的比特里，坏状态贡献的占 3000 / 3070。这就是全概率和贝叶斯的直观版。

详细解析(a) 全概率：分情况，再加权求和

  1. 做什么：把"出错"按发送时的状态分成两种情况。为什么：每种状态下的误码率已知，但不知道某个比特是在哪种状态下发的，只能两种都算、按出现概率加权。
  2. 怎么算：
    P(G) P(e | G) = 0.7 × 10⁻⁴ = 7 × 10⁻⁵
    P(B) P(e | B) = 0.3 × 10⁻² = 3 × 10⁻³
    Pe = 7 × 10⁻⁵ + 3 × 10⁻³ = 0.00007 + 0.003 = 3.07 × 10⁻³
  3. 计算器提示：两个数差两个数量级，先都写成小数再相加，不容易错位。

详细解析(b) 贝叶斯：由结果反推原因

做什么：已知"出错了"，求"当时是坏状态"的后验概率。为什么答案不是 0.3：0.3 是看到结果之前的先验；"出错"这个信息让坏状态的可能性大大增加，因为坏状态下出错的概率是好状态的 100 倍。

P(B | e) = P(B) P(e | B) / Pe = 3 × 10⁻³ / 3.07 × 10⁻³ = 0.977
P(G | e) = 7 × 10⁻⁵ / 3.07 × 10⁻³ = 0.023
检验：0.977 + 0.023 = 1

对比一下：如果知道这个比特没有出错，坏状态的概率 = 0.3 × 0.99 / (1 − 0.00307) = 0.298，几乎还是 0.3。"出错"带来的信息很多，"没出错"几乎不改变判断，因为两种状态下绝大多数比特都是对的。

知识讲解离散随机变量的期望和方差

离散随机变量（discrete random variable）只取有限几个值，每个值有一个概率。期望（expectation，也叫均值 mean，p.10，老师说要背）E[X] = Σ x PX(x)，就是"按概率加权的平均"。方差（variance，p.11）V[X] = E[(X − E[X])²] = E[X²] − (E[X])²，衡量取值离平均值有多分散；标准差是方差开根号，单位和 X 一样。

讲义 p.10 还有一句：一般来说 E[g(X)] ≠ g(E[X])（老师说这是往年常见错误）。dB 换算就是一个 g：g(γ) = 10 log₁₀γ。"先换成 dB 再平均"算的是 E[g(γ)]，"先平均再换成 dB"算的是 g(E[γ])，两者不相等。题目问"平均 SNR 用 dB 表示"，要的是后者。

详细解析(c) 先换线性值，再列表算

状态概率 pSNR（dB）线性 γγ·pγ²·p
好0.710 dB10770
坏0.30 dB10.30.3
合计17.370.3
10 dB → 10^(10/10) = 10；0 dB → 10⁰ = 1
E[γ] = 7 + 0.3 = 7.3
E[γ²] = 70 + 0.3 = 70.3
Var(γ) = 70.3 − 7.3² = 70.3 − 53.29 = 17.01；标准差 = √17.01 = 4.12
平均 SNR = 10 log₁₀7.3 = 10 × 0.8633 = 8.63 dB

检验（按定义算方差）：0.7 × (10 − 7.3)² + 0.3 × (1 − 7.3)² = 0.7 × 7.29 + 0.3 × 39.69 = 5.103 + 11.907 = 17.01，和上面一样。

易错常见丢分点

  • (b) 写成 0.3：把先验当成了后验。
  • 贝叶斯的分母只写了坏状态那一项，结果等于 1，显然不对；分母是 (a) 的总和。
  • 直接平均 dB 值：0.7 × 10 + 0.3 × 0 = 7 dB，相当于线性 5.01，比正确的 7.3 小。老师说没做 dB ↔ 线性换算会扣分。
  • 方差忘了减 (E[γ])²，写成 70.3；也不要把方差再换成 dB，方差没有 dB 的意义，题目也没问。
  • 10⁻⁴ 和 10⁻² 相加时数量级对错。

考试英文写法

Let G and B denote the good and bad states and e a bit error: P(G) = 0.7, P(B) = 0.3, P(e | G) = 10⁻⁴, P(e | B) = 10⁻².

(a) By the total probability theorem, Pe = P(G)P(e | G) + P(B)P(e | B) = 0.7 × 10⁻⁴ + 0.3 × 10⁻² = 3.07 × 10⁻³.

(b) By Bayes' rule, P(B | e) = P(B)P(e | B) / Pe = (3 × 10⁻³) / (3.07 × 10⁻³) = 0.977.

(c) In linear scale the SNR is 10 (10 dB) with probability 0.7 and 1 (0 dB) with probability 0.3. E[γ] = 0.7 × 10 + 0.3 × 1 = 7.3, i.e. 10 log₁₀7.3 = 8.63 dB. E[γ²] = 0.7 × 100 + 0.3 × 1 = 70.3, so Var(γ) = 70.3 − 7.3² = 17.01. The dB values must not be averaged directly, because E[g(X)] ≠ g(E[X]).

一句话：先列"状态—概率—条件概率"表；全概率是加权求和，贝叶斯是"其中一项 ÷ 总和"；求均值和方差前先把 dB 换成线性值。
◆ 本题知识点
考什么
全概率、贝叶斯（蓝框必背）、离散随机变量的期望和方差（第 1b 章 p.5–14，Quiz 范围）。
前置知识
P(B) = ΣP(Ai)P(B | Ai)；P(Ai | B) = P(Ai)P(B | Ai)/P(B)；E[X] = Σx·p(x)；Var(X) = E[X²] − (E[X])²。
解题套路
先列"状态—概率—条件概率"表；(a) 加权求和；(b) 用 (a) 里对应那一项除以 (a) 的总和；(c) 换成线性值后列表求 E[X]、E[X²]。
易错
直接平均 dB 值（得 7 dB，错）；方差忘了减均值的平方；贝叶斯的分母是全概率算出的总误码率；全概率必须对所有状态求和（老师点名的往年错误）。
直觉
坏状态只占 30% 的时间，却贡献了 97.7% 的错误：平均误码率几乎完全由信道差的时候决定，这也是第 4 章要用分集的原因。
举一反三
三个状态的信道同理，表里多一行；也可以问"连续两个比特都出错的概率"（独立时相乘）。
联系
Practice 4 第 2 题（离散 SNR 求平均误码率）；P2 Q2 (c) 是全概率的连续版本。

Approach: (a) Split into cases and weight them: total probability. (b) The result (an error) is known and we ask about the cause (the state): Bayes' rule. (c) Mean and variance of a discrete random variable, after converting dB to linear values.

  1. (a) Total probability: \(P_e=P(G)\,P(e\mid G)+P(B)\,P(e\mid B)=0.7\times10^{-4}+0.3\times10^{-2}=7\times10^{-5}+3\times10^{-3}\)
    Pe = 3.07 × 10⁻³
  2. (b) Bayes: \(P(B\mid e)=\dfrac{P(B)\,P(e\mid B)}{P_e}=\dfrac{3\times10^{-3}}{3.07\times10^{-3}}=0.977\). The numerator is the "bad" term of (a); the denominator is the answer of (a).
  3. (c) Convert to linear first: 10 dB → 10, 0 dB → 1.
    E[γ] = 0.7 × 10 + 0.3 × 1 = 7.3　　E[γ²] = 0.7 × 100 + 0.3 × 1 = 70.3
  4. \(\mathrm{Var}(\gamma)=E[\gamma^2]-(E[\gamma])^2=70.3-53.29=17.01\) (standard deviation 4.12); mean SNR \(=10\log_{10}7.3=8.63\) dB.
(a) 3.07 × 10⁻³; (b) 0.977; (c) E[γ] = 7.3 (8.63 dB), Var(γ) = 17.0
Official answer: none (self-written mock question); all numbers checked by src/p2build/verify_mock.py
Averaging the dB values gives 0.7 × 10 + 0.3 × 0 = 7 dB, which is wrong: dB is logarithmic, so convert to linear before averaging. The lecturer said a missing dB ↔ linear conversion loses marks. Part (b) is typical: the bad state occupies only 30% of the time but causes 97.7% of the errors.
Concept + step by step: total probability, Bayes, discrete mean and variance

ConceptConditional probability, total probability and Bayes are one toolkit

Conditional probability P(A | B) reads "the probability of A, given that B has happened". The bit error probabilities in this question are conditional probabilities: P(e | G) = 10⁻⁴ means "the probability that a bit is in error when the link is in the good state".

Multiplication rule (Ch 1b p.6): P(A ∩ B) = P(A) P(B | A). For example, the probability of "bad state and error" = P(B) P(e | B) = 0.3 × 10⁻² = 3 × 10⁻³.

Total probability theorem (p.8): split all possible cases A₁, A₂, … (non-overlapping, together covering everything), compute "this case happens and B happens" for each, then add them all: P(B) = Σ P(Ai) P(B | Ai). The slide calls it "divide and conquer".

Bayes' rule (p.9, blue box, memorise): given the result B, ask backwards about the cause Ai: \(P(A_i\mid B)=\dfrac{P(A_i)\,P(B\mid A_i)}{\sum_j P(A_j)\,P(B\mid A_j)}\). The numerator is the multiplication rule, the denominator is total probability. P(Ai) is the a priori probability and P(Ai | B) the a posteriori probability.

The lecturer stressed in class: in past years some students wrote P(B) as the single term P(A₁)P(B | A₁); total probability must sum over all cases. Bayes' rule comes back again and again in Ch 3 and Ch 6 (maximum likelihood detection, MLSE).

Turning probabilities into counts makes it easiest to see. Suppose 1 million bits are sent:

StateBits sentBits in error
Good (probability 0.7)0.7 million0.7 million × 10⁻⁴ = 70
Bad (probability 0.3)0.3 million0.3 million × 10⁻² = 3000
Total1 million3070

(a) The overall error rate = 3070 / 1 million; (b) among all bits in error, the share from the bad state is 3000 / 3070. That is the intuitive version of total probability and Bayes.

Step by step(a) Total probability: split into cases, then a weighted sum

  1. What: split "error" by the state at the time the bit was sent. Why: the error rate in each state is known, but we do not know which state a given bit was sent in, so we compute both and weight them by how often each state occurs.
  2. How:
    P(G) P(e | G) = 0.7 × 10⁻⁴ = 7 × 10⁻⁵
    P(B) P(e | B) = 0.3 × 10⁻² = 3 × 10⁻³
    Pe = 7 × 10⁻⁵ + 3 × 10⁻³ = 0.00007 + 0.003 = 3.07 × 10⁻³
  3. Calculator hint: the two numbers differ by two orders of magnitude; write both as decimals before adding so the digits line up.

Step by step(b) Bayes: from the result back to the cause

What: knowing "an error happened", find the a posteriori probability that the link was in the bad state. Why the answer is not 0.3: 0.3 is the a priori probability before seeing the result; the information "error" makes the bad state much more likely, because errors are 100 times more likely in the bad state than in the good state.

P(B | e) = P(B) P(e | B) / Pe = 3 × 10⁻³ / 3.07 × 10⁻³ = 0.977
P(G | e) = 7 × 10⁻⁵ / 3.07 × 10⁻³ = 0.023
Check: 0.977 + 0.023 = 1

For comparison: if we know the bit was not in error, the probability of the bad state = 0.3 × 0.99 / (1 − 0.00307) = 0.298, still almost 0.3. "Error" carries a lot of information; "no error" hardly changes the picture, because almost all bits are correct in both states.

ConceptMean and variance of a discrete random variable

A discrete random variable takes only a few values, each with a probability. The expectation (mean, p.10, the lecturer said to memorise it) E[X] = Σ x PX(x) is the probability-weighted average. The variance (p.11) V[X] = E[(X − E[X])²] = E[X²] − (E[X])² measures how spread out the values are around the mean; the standard deviation is its square root and has the same unit as X.

Slide p.10 also says: in general E[g(X)] ≠ g(E[X]) (the lecturer said this is a common past mistake). Converting to dB is such a g: g(γ) = 10 log₁₀γ. "Convert to dB, then average" computes E[g(γ)]; "average, then convert to dB" computes g(E[γ]); they are not equal. The question asks for the mean SNR expressed in dB, which is the second one.

Step by step(c) Convert to linear first, then use a table

StateProbability pSNR (dB)Linear γγ·pγ²·p
Good0.710 dB10770
Bad0.30 dB10.30.3
Total17.370.3
10 dB → 10^(10/10) = 10; 0 dB → 10⁰ = 1
E[γ] = 7 + 0.3 = 7.3
E[γ²] = 70 + 0.3 = 70.3
Var(γ) = 70.3 − 7.3² = 70.3 − 53.29 = 17.01; standard deviation = √17.01 = 4.12
mean SNR = 10 log₁₀7.3 = 10 × 0.8633 = 8.63 dB

Check (variance from the definition): 0.7 × (10 − 7.3)² + 0.3 × (1 − 7.3)² = 0.7 × 7.29 + 0.3 × 39.69 = 5.103 + 11.907 = 17.01, the same as above.

PitfallsWhere marks are usually lost

  • (b) answered as 0.3: the a priori probability taken as the a posteriori one.
  • Only the bad-state term in the Bayes denominator, giving 1, which is clearly wrong; the denominator is the total from (a).
  • Averaging the dB values directly: 0.7 × 10 + 0.3 × 0 = 7 dB, i.e. 5.01 in linear scale, smaller than the correct 7.3. The lecturer said a missing dB ↔ linear conversion loses marks.
  • Forgetting to subtract (E[γ])² in the variance and writing 70.3; also do not convert the variance to dB, it has no meaning in dB and the question does not ask for it.
  • Misaligning the orders of magnitude when adding 10⁻⁴ and 10⁻² terms.

ExamHow to write it in English

Let G and B denote the good and bad states and e a bit error: P(G) = 0.7, P(B) = 0.3, P(e | G) = 10⁻⁴, P(e | B) = 10⁻².

(a) By the total probability theorem, Pe = P(G)P(e | G) + P(B)P(e | B) = 0.7 × 10⁻⁴ + 0.3 × 10⁻² = 3.07 × 10⁻³.

(b) By Bayes' rule, P(B | e) = P(B)P(e | B) / Pe = (3 × 10⁻³) / (3.07 × 10⁻³) = 0.977.

(c) In linear scale the SNR is 10 (10 dB) with probability 0.7 and 1 (0 dB) with probability 0.3. E[γ] = 0.7 × 10 + 0.3 × 1 = 7.3, i.e. 10 log₁₀7.3 = 8.63 dB. E[γ²] = 0.7 × 100 + 0.3 × 1 = 70.3, so Var(γ) = 70.3 − 7.3² = 17.01. The dB values must not be averaged directly, because E[g(X)] ≠ g(E[X]).

In one line: first make a "state – probability – conditional probability" table; total probability is a weighted sum, Bayes is "one term ÷ the total"; convert dB to linear before taking the mean and variance.
◆ Key points
What it tests
Total probability, Bayes' rule (blue box, memorise), mean and variance of a discrete random variable (Ch 1b p.5–14, Quiz scope).
Background
P(B) = ΣP(Ai)P(B | Ai); P(Ai | B) = P(Ai)P(B | Ai)/P(B); E[X] = Σx·p(x); Var(X) = E[X²] − (E[X])².
Method
Make a table of state, probability and conditional probability. (a) Weighted sum; (b) the matching term of (a) divided by the total of (a); (c) convert to linear, then E[X] and E[X²] from the table.
Common mistakes
Averaging dB values (7 dB, wrong); forgetting to subtract the squared mean; the Bayes denominator is the total error probability from total probability; total probability must sum over all states (a past mistake the lecturer pointed out).
Intuition
The bad state is 30% of the time but gives 97.7% of the errors: the average error rate is set almost entirely by the bad periods, which is why Ch 4 uses diversity.
Variations
A three-state channel works the same with one more row; or "probability that two consecutive bits are both wrong" (multiply if independent).
Link
Practice 4 Q2 (average error rate over a discrete SNR); P2 Q2 (c) is the continuous version of total probability.
模拟 A · Q2

简化路径损耗模型 + 阴影：中断概率与最大覆盖距离

Original question · 中英对照Mock Quiz A (self-written)

A base station transmits 100 mW. Propagation follows the simplified path loss model \(P_r=P_tK(d_0/d)^{\gamma}\) with \(K_{dB}=-31.5\) dB, \(d_0=1\) m and \(\gamma=3.6\). The receiver needs a received power of at least −110 dBm to avoid outage. Log-normal shadowing with a standard deviation of 6 dB is present.

基站发射功率为 100 mW。传播服从简化路径损耗模型 \(P_r=P_tK(d_0/d)^{\gamma}\)，其中 \(K_{dB}=-31.5\) dB，\(d_0=1\) m，\(\gamma=3.6\)。接收功率至少要 −110 dBm 才不中断。存在对数正态阴影，标准差为 6 dB。

(a) Write the model in dB form and find the average received power at d = 300 m. (3 marks)

(a) 写出模型的 dB 形式，并求 d = 300 m 处的平均接收功率。（3 分）

(b) Find the outage probability at d = 300 m. (3 marks)

(b) 求 d = 300 m 处的中断概率。（3 分）

(c) Find the maximum distance at which the outage probability does not exceed 1%. (4 marks)

(c) 求中断概率不超过 1% 的最大距离。（4 分）

简化路径损耗模型（必背）P_out = Q(余量/σ)（必背）Q 函数反查讲义第 2 章 p.8、p.12–16
题意讲解：这道题到底要干嘛

场景题目在讲一件什么事

基站以 100 mW 发射，信号随距离衰减（简化路径损耗模型），再叠加随机的阴影起伏。手机要收到至少 −110 dBm 才能通信。

题目先问 300 m 处平均能收到多少、断线概率多大，再反过来问：要让断线概率不超过 1%，手机最远能离多远。

符号题目里每个量是什么

100 mW发射功率，先换成 20 dBm
\(K_{dB}=-31.5\) dB，\(d_0=1\) m模型常数和参考距离
γ = 3.6路径损耗指数：距离每大 10 倍，多损耗 36 dB
−110 dBm门限：低于它就中断
σ = 6 dB阴影的标准差

要求每一问在问什么

  • (a) 写出模型的 dB 形式，代 d = 300 m 求平均接收功率。
  • (b) 平均功率比门限高多少（余量），再求阴影把功率压低超过余量的概率：Q(余量/σ)。
  • (c) 反过来：中断 ≤ 1% 需要多大的余量，平均功率至少多大，对应的距离最远多少。

注意读题容易误会的地方

  • 100 mW 要先换成 dBm，老师说没换会扣分。
  • (c) 是 Q 函数反查：Q(x) = 0.01 → x = 2.326。

目的这道题想让你明白什么

路径损耗决定平均功率，阴影决定围绕平均值的起伏；中断概率只取决于"余量是几个标准差"。覆盖距离就是由这个关系反推出来的。

一句话：用路径损耗模型算 300 m 处的平均功率和中断概率，再反推中断 ≤ 1% 时的最大覆盖距离。

思路：先把 mW 换成 dBm，用 dB 形式算平均接收功率；平均功率比门限高出的部分就是余量，中断概率 = Q(余量/σ)；(c) 反过来：由 1% 反查 Q 得到需要的余量，再解出距离。

  1. 先换单位：\(P_t=10\log_{10}100=20\) dBm（没换成 dBm 会扣分）。
  2. (a) dB 形式：\(P_{r,dB}=P_{t,dB}+K_{dB}-10\gamma\log_{10}(d/d_0)\)。
    P_r(300 m) = 20 − 31.5 − 36 × log₁₀300 = 20 − 31.5 − 36 × 2.477 = −100.68 dBm
  3. (b) 余量 = −100.68 − (−110) = 9.32 dB。
    P_out = Q(9.32 / 6) = Q(1.55) = 0.060
  4. (c) 要求 \(Q(x)\le0.01\)，反查 Q 表得 x ≥ 2.326，余量至少 2.326 × 6 = 13.96 dB，所以平均功率至少 −110 + 13.96 = −96.04 dBm。
  5. 解距离：\(20-31.5-36\log_{10}d\ge-96.04\ \Rightarrow\ 36\log_{10}d\le84.54\ \Rightarrow\ \log_{10}d\le2.348\)
    d_max = 10^2.348 ≈ 223 m
(a) P_r,dB = P_t,dB + K_dB − 36 log₁₀d，300 m 处 ≈ −100.7 dBm；(b) P_out ≈ 6.0%；(c) d_max ≈ 223 m
官方答案：无（自编模拟题）。所有数字已用 src/p2build/verify_mock.py 验算。
300 m 处中断 6%，远高于 1% 的常用目标。要压到 1%，要么把覆盖半径缩到约 223 m，要么把发射功率提高 13.96 − 9.32 = 4.64 dB。
逐步详解：dB 和 dBm，每个量是什么单位

区别dBm 是"有多少"，dB 是"变了多少倍"

dBdBm
表示什么两个功率的比值（倍数），没有单位绝对功率，以 1 mW 为参考
定义10 log₁₀(P₁/P₂)10 log₁₀(P / 1 mW)
例子放大 100 倍 = +20 dB；衰减到 1/1000 = −30 dB100 mW = 20 dBm；1 mW = 0 dBm

K为什么是 dB，不是 dBm

线性式 \(P_r=P_t\cdot K\cdot(d_0/d)^{\gamma}\) 里，\(P_r\)、\(P_t\) 是功率（mW），K 和 \((d_0/d)^{\gamma}\) 是乘在功率上的倍数，没有单位。K 的意思是"在参考距离 d₀ 处，接收功率是发射功率的多少倍"，所以是比值，取对数后是 dB。\(K_{dB}=-31.5\) dB 表示 1 m 处信号衰减到发射功率的 \(10^{-3.15}\approx1/1400\)。

取 dB 后乘法变加法，只有 \(P_t\) 带 dBm，其余都是 dB，结果自然是 dBm：

\[\underbrace{P_{r,dBm}}_{\text{dBm}}=\underbrace{P_{t,dBm}}_{\text{dBm}}+\underbrace{K_{dB}}_{\text{dB}}-\underbrace{10\gamma\log_{10}(d/d_0)}_{\text{dB}}\]
运算结果意思
dBm + dBdBm功率乘一个倍数，还是功率
dBm − dBmdB两个功率相除得倍数（余量、SNR）
dB + dBdB倍数乘倍数，还是倍数
dBm + dBm没有意义功率乘功率不是功率

反过来验证：如果 K 也是 dBm，右边就成了 dBm + dBm，相当于"功率 × 功率"，没有物理意义。

本题每个量的单位

量值单位
发射功率100 mW = 20dBm（绝对功率）
K−31.5dB（比值）
路径损耗项 36 log₁₀30089.18dB（比值）
平均接收功率−100.68dBm
门限−110dBm
余量 = 接收功率 − 门限9.32dB（dBm − dBm）
阴影标准差 σ6dB（围绕平均值的起伏，也是比值）

Practice 1 第 2 题原文把 σ 写成 "dBm"，严格说应该是 dB。

一句话：dBm 是绝对功率，dB 是比值。路径损耗模型里只有发射功率和接收功率是 dBm；K、路径损耗、阴影、余量都是 dB。
◆ 本题知识点
考什么
简化路径损耗模型的 dB 形式（必背）、阴影中断概率 Pout = Q(余量/σ)（必背）、Q 函数反查（第 2 章 p.8、p.12–16）。
前置知识
Pr,dB = Pt,dB + KdB − 10γ log₁₀(d/d₀)；mW → dBm：10 log₁₀(mW 数)；Q(2.326) = 0.01。
解题套路
① 换单位；② 代模型得平均功率；③ 余量 = 平均功率 − 门限；④ Pout = Q(余量/σ)。反求距离时倒过来：中断要求 → 反查 x → 余量 = xσ → 平均功率下限 → 解 d。
关键一步
(c) 余量 13.96 dB → 平均功率 ≥ −96.04 dBm → 36 log₁₀d ≤ 84.54 → d ≤ 223 m。
易错
100 mW 没换成 20 dBm 就代（会扣分）；10γ = 36，别写成 3.6；Q 的参数是余量除以 σ，不是除以 σ²。
举一反三
给定距离求所需发射功率；或给定中断概率求允许的最大 σ（2023-24 S2 Q1(a)(ii) 就这样问）。
联系
Practice 1 第 2 题（正算、反算中断概率）；Practice 1 第 1 题 (b)。

Approach: Convert mW to dBm and use the dB form for the average received power. The amount by which the average exceeds the threshold is the margin, and outage = Q(margin/σ). For (c), work backwards: 1% → inverse Q → required margin → distance.

  1. Units first: \(P_t=10\log_{10}100=20\) dBm (leaving it in mW loses marks).
  2. (a) dB form: \(P_{r,dB}=P_{t,dB}+K_{dB}-10\gamma\log_{10}(d/d_0)\).
    Pr(300 m) = 20 − 31.5 − 36 × log₁₀300 = 20 − 31.5 − 36 × 2.477 = −100.68 dBm
  3. (b) Margin = −100.68 − (−110) = 9.32 dB.
    Pout = Q(9.32 / 6) = Q(1.55) = 0.060
  4. (c) Need \(Q(x)\le0.01\); the Q table gives x ≥ 2.326, so the margin must be at least 2.326 × 6 = 13.96 dB and the average power at least −110 + 13.96 = −96.04 dBm.
  5. Solve for the distance: \(20-31.5-36\log_{10}d\ge-96.04\ \Rightarrow\ 36\log_{10}d\le84.54\ \Rightarrow\ \log_{10}d\le2.348\)
    dmax = 102.348 ≈ 223 m
(a) Pr,dB = Pt,dB + KdB − 36 log₁₀d, ≈ −100.7 dBm at 300 m; (b) Pout ≈ 6.0%; (c) dmax ≈ 223 m
Official answer: none (self-written mock question); all numbers checked by src/p2build/verify_mock.py
At 300 m the outage is 6%, far above the usual 1% target. To reach 1%, either shrink the coverage radius to about 223 m or raise the transmit power by 13.96 − 9.32 = 4.64 dB.
Step by step: dB versus dBm, and the unit of every quantity

DifferencedBm is "how much", dB is "how many times"

dBdBm
MeaningRatio of two powers, no unitAbsolute power, referenced to 1 mW
Definition10 log₁₀(P₁/P₂)10 log₁₀(P / 1 mW)
Examples×100 = +20 dB; down to 1/1000 = −30 dB100 mW = 20 dBm; 1 mW = 0 dBm

KWhy K is in dB, not dBm

In the linear form \(P_r=P_t\cdot K\cdot(d_0/d)^{\gamma}\), \(P_r\) and \(P_t\) are powers (mW) while K and \((d_0/d)^{\gamma}\) are unitless factors multiplying the power. K means "how many times the transmit power is received at the reference distance d₀", a ratio, so in log form it is in dB. \(K_{dB}=-31.5\) dB means the signal at 1 m is \(10^{-3.15}\approx1/1400\) of the transmit power.

Taking dB turns products into sums; only \(P_t\) is in dBm and everything else is in dB, so the result is in dBm:

\[\underbrace{P_{r,dBm}}_{\text{dBm}}=\underbrace{P_{t,dBm}}_{\text{dBm}}+\underbrace{K_{dB}}_{\text{dB}}-\underbrace{10\gamma\log_{10}(d/d_0)}_{\text{dB}}\]
OperationResultMeaning
dBm + dBdBmA power times a factor is still a power
dBm − dBmdBA ratio of two powers (margin, SNR)
dB + dBdBA factor times a factor is still a factor
dBm + dBmMeaninglessPower times power is not a power

Check: if K were in dBm, the right-hand side would be dBm + dBm, i.e. "power × power", which has no physical meaning.

HereUnit of each quantity

QuantityValueUnit
Transmit power100 mW = 20dBm (absolute power)
K−31.5dB (ratio)
Path loss term 36 log₁₀30089.18dB (ratio)
Average received power−100.68dBm
Threshold−110dBm
Margin = received power − threshold9.32dB (dBm − dBm)
Shadowing standard deviation σ6dB (a fluctuation about the mean, also a ratio)

Practice 1 Q2 writes σ in "dBm"; strictly it should be dB.

In one line: dBm is an absolute power, dB is a ratio. In the path loss model only the transmit and received powers are in dBm; K, path loss, shadowing and margin are all in dB.
◆ Key points
What it tests
dB form of the simplified path loss model (memorise), shadowing outage Pout = Q(margin/σ) (memorise), inverse Q lookup (Ch 2 p.8, p.12–16).
Background
Pr,dB = Pt,dB + KdB − 10γ log₁₀(d/d₀); mW → dBm: 10 log₁₀(value in mW); Q(2.326) = 0.01.
Method
① Convert units; ② average power from the model; ③ margin = average − threshold; ④ Pout = Q(margin/σ). For the distance, reverse it: outage target → inverse Q → margin = xσ → minimum average power → solve for d.
Key step
(c) Margin 13.96 dB → average power ≥ −96.04 dBm → 36 log₁₀d ≤ 84.54 → d ≤ 223 m.
Common mistakes
Using 100 mW without converting to 20 dBm (loses marks); 10γ = 36, not 3.6; Q takes margin over σ, not over σ².
Variations
Required transmit power for a given distance; or the largest allowed σ for a given outage (asked in 2023-24 S2 Q1(a)(ii)).
Link
Practice 1 Q2 (outage forwards and backwards); Practice 1 Q1 (b).
模拟 A · Q3

离散时延谱、多普勒、衰落类型与 AFD

Original question · 中英对照Mock Quiz A (self-written)

A mobile channel has the discrete power delay profile below. The system transmits 50 k symbols/s on a 1.8 GHz carrier, and the mobile moves at 72 km/h.

某移动信道的离散功率时延谱如下表。系统在 1.8 GHz 载频上以 50 k 符号/秒发送，移动台速度为 72 km/h。

Delay τ (µs)
时延		Relative power (dB)
相对功率
00
1−3
3−6

(a) Find the mean delay and the rms delay spread of the channel. (3 marks)

(a) 求该信道的平均时延和 rms 时延扩展。（3 分）

(b) Find the maximum Doppler shift and the coherence time. Hence classify the fading experienced by the signal (flat or frequency-selective, fast or slow), and justify your answer. (4 marks)

(b) 求最大多普勒频移和相干时间，并据此判断信号经历的衰落类型（平坦还是频率选择性，快还是慢），说明理由。（4 分）

(c) For acceptable performance the received power must not fall more than 10 dB below its rms value. Assuming Rayleigh fading, find the average fade duration and the average number of symbols affected by one fade. The average fade duration is \(\bar\tau=\dfrac{e^{\rho^2}-1}{\sqrt{2\pi}f_m\rho}\). (3 marks)

(c) 为保证性能，接收功率不能比其 rms 值低 10 dB 以上。假设为瑞利衰落，求平均衰落时长，以及一次衰落平均影响多少个符号。平均衰落时长为 \(\bar\tau=\dfrac{e^{\rho^2}-1}{\sqrt{2\pi}f_m\rho}\)。（3 分）

平均时延 / rms 时延扩展σ/T_s < 0.1（必背）多普勒与相干时间AFD讲义第 2 章 p.30–43
题意讲解：这道题到底要干嘛

场景题目在讲一件什么事

信号经三条路径到达：直射（0 µs）、晚 1 µs、晚 3 µs，功率依次弱一些。手机以 72 km/h 移动，载频 1.8 GHz，符号速率 50 k 符号/秒。

题目从三个角度看这个信道：回声拖多长（时延扩展）、信道变多快（多普勒），以及一次深衰落持续多久（AFD）。

符号题目里每个量是什么

功率时延谱每条路径的时延和相对功率（dB），算的时候换成线性值当权重
μ、σ平均时延和 rms 时延扩展
\(f_m\)最大多普勒频移 = v fc/c
\(T_C\approx1/f_m\)相干时间：信道基本不变的时长
ρAFD 公式里的门限与 rms 值之比，是幅度比

要求每一问在问什么

  • (a) 按功率加权，求时延的均值和标准差。
  • (b) 求 fm 和 TC，然后判断衰落类型：σ 和 Ts 比决定平坦还是频率选择性；TC 和 Ts 比决定快还是慢。两个维度都要答。
  • (c) "功率不能比 rms 值低 10 dB 以上"给出门限，换成幅度比 ρ，代 AFD 公式，再看一次衰落覆盖几个符号。

注意读题容易误会的地方

  • 功率低 10 dB 对应幅度比 ρ = √0.1 ≈ 0.316，不是 0.1。
  • 问衰落类型只答一个维度只给一半分（老师说过）。
  • 72 km/h 要换成 20 m/s。

目的这道题想让你明白什么

时延扩展决定频率维度（平坦/选择性），多普勒决定时间维度（快/慢），两者独立；AFD 告诉你一次深衰落会吞掉多少个符号。

一句话：三径信道：(a) 求时延均值和 rms 扩展，(b) 求多普勒和相干时间并判断衰落类型，(c) 求门限低 10 dB 时的平均衰落时长和受影响的符号数。

思路：(a) 把各条径的功率（线性值）当权重，求时延的加权均值和标准差；(b) 两个维度分开判断：σ 对比 Ts 定平坦 / 选择性，TC 对比 Ts 定快 / 慢；(c) 先由"功率低 10 dB"求出幅度比 ρ，再代 AFD 公式。

  1. (a) 先换线性：0、−3、−6 dB → 1、0.501、0.251，总和 1.752。
    μ = (0 × 1 + 1 × 0.501 + 3 × 0.251) / 1.752 = 1.255 / 1.752 = 0.716 µs
  2. E[τ²] = (0 + 1 × 0.501 + 9 × 0.251) / 1.752 = 2.762 / 1.752 = 1.576 µs²　　σ = √(1.576 − 0.716²) = √1.063 = 1.03 µs
  3. (b) 平坦 / 选择性：\(T_s=1/50\text{k}=20\) µs，\(\sigma/T_s=1.03/20=0.052\lt0.1\) → 平坦衰落。
  4. 快 / 慢：v = 72 km/h = 20 m/s，\(f_m=vf_c/c=20\times1.8\times10^9/3\times10^8=120\) Hz，\(T_C\approx1/f_m=8.33\) ms ≫ \(T_s\) = 20 µs → 慢衰落。结论：平坦慢衰落。
  5. (c) 功率低 10 dB，幅度比 \(\rho=\sqrt{0.1}=0.316\)（ρ 是幅度比，不是功率比）。
    AFD = (e^0.1 − 1) / (√(2π) × 120 × 0.316) = 0.1052 / 95.1 = 1.11 ms
  6. 每次衰落影响的符号数 ≈ 1.11 ms / 20 µs ≈ 55 个。
(a) μ ≈ 0.72 µs，σ ≈ 1.03 µs；(b) f_m = 120 Hz，T_C ≈ 8.3 ms，平坦慢衰落；(c) AFD ≈ 1.1 ms，每次约 55 个符号
官方答案：无（自编模拟题）。所有数字已用 src/p2build/verify_mock.py 验算。
如果把 ρ 当成 0.1，AFD 只有 0.33 ms，差了 3 倍多。顺便可以算 LCR = √(2π) × 120 × 0.316 × e−0.1 ≈ 86 次/秒；AFD × LCR = 1 − e−0.1 ≈ 0.095，约 9.5% 的时间低于门限。
逐步详解：每个量是什么、怎么算、哪些要背

背不背这道题用到的公式（按老师第 8–10 周录音）

公式用在要不要背
平均时延 μ、rms 时延扩展 σ(a)考试会给；要会按功率加权去算
\(\sigma_{T_m}/T_s\lt0.1\) → 平坦(b)要背（蓝框）
\(f_m=vf_c/c\)、\(T_C\approx1/f_m\)(b)录音里没点名；公式很短、题目常要先算它，建议记住
AFD、LCR(c)考试会给（本题题干也给了）；要会说出 ρ = Zmin/Zrms、fm 是最大多普勒频移
功率 ∝ 幅度²(c)基本关系，必须会

瑞利衰落本身：要背 SNR 的指数分布 \(p(\gamma)=\frac{1}{\bar\gamma}e^{-\gamma/\bar\gamma}\) 和瑞利中断 \(1-e^{-\gamma_0/\bar\gamma}\)（老师说"经常出现"）；包络的瑞利 PDF 不用背；瑞利分布的推导不会让推；衰落余量公式尽量记。

(a)平均时延和 rms 时延扩展是什么

基站发一个很短的脉冲，手机收到的是先后到达的几份"回声"：0 µs 到的最强（0 dB），1 µs 到的弱一点（−3 dB），3 µs 到的最弱（−6 dB）。这张表就是功率时延谱。平均时延 μ 是回声能量的"重心"在哪个时刻；rms 时延扩展 σ 是能量在时间上散得多开。真正重要的是 σ，它决定回声会不会拖进下一个符号。

为什么要除以 1.752：这是按功率加权的平均，加权平均要除以权重之和。就像按学分算平均成绩，要除以总学分。三条路径的功率（线性值）是 1、0.501、0.251，加起来 1.752。

换个角度：除以 1.752 就是把功率变成"能量占比"：1/1.752 = 0.571，0.501/1.752 = 0.286，0.251/1.752 = 0.143，加起来是 1，相当于一个离散概率分布。于是 μ = 0 × 0.571 + 1 × 0.286 + 3 × 0.143 = 0.716 µs，σ 就是这个分布的标准差。这和第 1b 章离散随机变量的期望、方差是同一套算法。

  • 权重必须用线性值，dB 值（0、−3、−6）是负数，不能当权重。
  • σ 不是"最大时延 − 最小时延"：这里时延差 3 µs，σ 只有 1.03 µs，因为晚到的回声功率小、权重低。

(b)衰落类型：两个维度分别判断

维度问的问题比较本题
平坦 / 频率选择性回声会不会拖进下一个符号σ 和 Ts：σ/Ts < 0.1 → 平坦1.03/20 = 0.052 → 平坦
慢 / 快一个符号的时间里信道变没变TC 和 Ts：Ts ≪ TC → 慢20 µs ≪ 8.33 ms → 慢

两个维度互不相关：时延扩展只管平坦 / 选择性，多普勒只管快 / 慢。符号时长被夹在中间：\(\sigma_{T_m}\ll T_s\ll T_C\) 时就是平坦慢衰落。只答一个维度只给一半分（老师说过）。σ/Ts 只比 0.1 稍大（比如 0.15）时不能下结论，要远大于 0.1 才能说是频率选择性。

(b)最大多普勒频移和相干时间

最大多普勒频移 fm：手机在动，收到的频率会偏一点（和救护车开过来时警笛变尖是同一个道理）。频移 \(f_d=\frac{v}{c}f_c\cos\theta\)，正对着信号方向走时 cos θ = 1，频移最大：\(f_m=vf_c/c\)。本题 v = 72 km/h ÷ 3.6 = 20 m/s，\(f_m=20\times1.8\times10^9/3\times10^8=120\) Hz。

相干时间 TC：信道基本保持不变的时长。多普勒越大，各条回波的相位变得越快，信道变化越快，TC 越短：\(T_C\approx1/f_m=1/120=8.33\) ms。两者互为倒数：走得越快，fm 越大，TC 越短；不动时 fm = 0，信道不变。

  • km/h 要除以 3.6 换成 m/s；GHz 换成 Hz；比较 TC 和 Ts 前把 ms、µs 统一。

(c)ρ 怎么来：先功率比，再开根号

ρ = Zmin/Zrms 是幅度之比，题目给的却是功率条件（"功率不能比 rms 值低 10 dB 以上"）。功率是幅度的平方（P = V²/R），所以幅度是功率的平方根：

\[\frac{P_{\min}}{P_{\mathrm{rms}}}=10^{-10/10}=0.1=\Big(\frac{Z_{\min}}{Z_{\mathrm{rms}}}\Big)^2=\rho^2\ \Rightarrow\ \rho=\sqrt{0.1}=0.316\]
幅度比 ρ功率比 ρ²功率低多少
0.7070.53 dB
0.3160.110 dB
0.10.0120 dB

功率的 dB 除以 10，幅度的 dB 除以 20：\(\rho=10^{-10/20}=0.316\)。AFD 公式指数里的 ρ² 正好就是功率比 0.1，可以直接用。和 Practice 2 第 1 题对比：那里说 "signal strength is 0.1 of the rms value"，指的是幅度，ρ 直接等于 0.1。题目说幅度（strength、envelope）就直接用；说功率或给 dB，就先换功率比再开根号。把本题 ρ 当成 0.1，AFD 会算成 0.33 ms，差 3 倍多。

(c)为什么除以 20 µs

20 µs 是一个符号的时长：50 k 符号/秒 → \(T_s=1/50000=20\) µs。一次深衰落平均持续 1.11 ms = 1110 µs，这段时间里有 1110 / 20 ≈ 55 个符号，所以一次衰落平均影响约 55 个连续符号。也可以写成 AFD × 符号速率 = 1.11×10⁻³ × 50×10³ ≈ 55。

这说明错误是成片出现的，不是零星的，所以要用交织把连续的比特打散，再靠纠错码纠正。

考试(b)(c) 的英文写法

Flat or frequency-selective: \(\sigma_{T_m}/T_s=1.03/20=0.052\lt0.1\), so the fading is flat.

Fast or slow: \(f_m=vf_c/c=120\) Hz, \(T_C\approx1/f_m=8.33\) ms ≫ \(T_s\) = 20 µs, so the channel is almost constant over a symbol and the fading is slow. Hence the signal experiences flat, slow fading.

(c) A power 10 dB below the rms value means \(\rho^2=0.1\), \(\rho=\sqrt{0.1}=0.316\). AFD \(=(e^{0.1}-1)/(\sqrt{2\pi}\times120\times0.316)=1.11\) ms. Symbols per fade \(=\text{AFD}/T_s=1.11\text{ ms}/20\ \mu s\approx55\).

一句话：功率加权求 μ、σ（除以总功率）→ σ 和 Ts 比定平坦/选择性，TC = 1/fm 和 Ts 比定快/慢 → 功率比开根号得 ρ → AFD ÷ Ts 得受影响的符号数。
◆ 本题知识点
考什么
离散功率时延谱的 μ、σ（第 2 章 p.30）；按平坦/选择性和快/慢两个维度判断衰落类型（p.33–36）；平均衰落时长（p.42–43）。
前置知识
功率加权：μ = ΣPiτi/ΣPi，σ = √(E[τ²] − μ²)；σ/Ts < 0.1 → 平坦（必背）；fm = v fc/c，TC ≈ 1/fm；ρ = 门限幅度 / rms 幅度。
解题套路
(a) dB → 线性 → 列表求加权均值和二阶矩；(b) Ts 分别和 σ、TC 比；(c) 功率比开根号得 ρ → 代 AFD → 除以 Ts 得符号数。
易错
拿 dB 值当权重；"功率低 10 dB"对应 ρ = √0.1，不是 0.1；问衰落类型只答一个维度只给一半分（老师说过），平坦/选择性和快/慢都要答；km/h 要换成 m/s。
检验
AFD × LCR = 1 − e−ρ² = 0.095：信号约 9.5% 的时间低于门限。
举一反三
符号速率提高到 200 k 符号/秒：Ts = 5 µs，σ/Ts = 0.21，就不能再说是平坦衰落了。
联系
Practice 1 第 3 题（连续时延谱）；Practice 2 第 1 题（AFD）；2025-26 S2 Q1(a)(i)(ii)。

Approach: (a) Use the linear power of each path as a weight and find the weighted mean and standard deviation of the delay. (b) Judge two dimensions separately: σ against Ts for flat / frequency-selective, TC against Ts for fast / slow. (c) Get the amplitude ratio ρ from "10 dB below" first, then use the AFD formula.

  1. (a) Linear powers: 0, −3, −6 dB → 1, 0.501, 0.251; sum 1.752.
    μ = (0 × 1 + 1 × 0.501 + 3 × 0.251) / 1.752 = 1.255 / 1.752 = 0.716 µs
  2. E[τ²] = (0 + 1 × 0.501 + 9 × 0.251) / 1.752 = 2.762 / 1.752 = 1.576 µs²　　σ = √(1.576 − 0.716²) = √1.063 = 1.03 µs
  3. (b) Flat or selective: \(T_s=1/50\text{k}=20\) µs and \(\sigma/T_s=1.03/20=0.052\lt0.1\) → flat fading.
  4. Fast or slow: v = 72 km/h = 20 m/s, \(f_m=vf_c/c=20\times1.8\times10^9/3\times10^8=120\) Hz, \(T_C\approx1/f_m=8.33\) ms ≫ \(T_s\) = 20 µs → slow fading. Conclusion: flat, slow fading.
  5. (c) Power 10 dB down means an amplitude ratio \(\rho=\sqrt{0.1}=0.316\) (ρ is an amplitude ratio, not a power ratio).
    AFD = (e0.1 − 1) / (√(2π) × 120 × 0.316) = 0.1052 / 95.1 = 1.11 ms
  6. Symbols affected per fade ≈ 1.11 ms / 20 µs ≈ 55.
(a) μ ≈ 0.72 µs, σ ≈ 1.03 µs; (b) fm = 120 Hz, TC ≈ 8.3 ms, flat slow fading; (c) AFD ≈ 1.1 ms, about 55 symbols per fade
Official answer: none (self-written mock question); all numbers checked by src/p2build/verify_mock.py
Taking ρ = 0.1 by mistake gives AFD = 0.33 ms, more than 3 times too small. Also LCR = √(2π) × 120 × 0.316 × e−0.1 ≈ 86 per second, and AFD × LCR = 1 − e−0.1 ≈ 0.095: the signal is below the threshold about 9.5% of the time.
Step by step: what each quantity is, how to compute it, what to memorise

Memorise?Formulas used here (from the lecturer's week 8–10 recordings)

FormulaPartMemorise?
Mean delay μ, rms delay spread σ(a)Given in the exam; know how to weight by power
\(\sigma_{T_m}/T_s\lt0.1\) → flat(b)Memorise (blue box)
\(f_m=vf_c/c\), \(T_C\approx1/f_m\)(b)Not named in the recordings; short and often needed first, so worth remembering
AFD, LCR(c)Given (this question gives AFD); be able to say ρ = Zmin/Zrms and that fm is the maximum Doppler shift
Power ∝ amplitude²(c)Basic relation, must know

Rayleigh fading itself: memorise the exponential SNR distribution \(p(\gamma)=\frac{1}{\bar\gamma}e^{-\gamma/\bar\gamma}\) and the Rayleigh outage \(1-e^{-\gamma_0/\bar\gamma}\) (the lecturer said it "often appears"); the Rayleigh envelope PDF need not be memorised; its derivation will not be asked; try to remember the fade-margin formula.

(a)What mean delay and rms delay spread are

A very short pulse arrives as several echoes: the strongest at 0 µs (0 dB), a weaker one at 1 µs (−3 dB), the weakest at 3 µs (−6 dB). That table is the power delay profile. The mean delay μ is the "centre of gravity" of the echo energy in time; the rms delay spread σ is how widely the energy is spread. σ is what matters: it decides whether echoes leak into the next symbol.

Why divide by 1.752: this is a power-weighted average, and a weighted average is divided by the sum of the weights, just like a credit-weighted grade average is divided by the total credits. The linear powers are 1, 0.501 and 0.251, which sum to 1.752.

Equivalently, dividing by 1.752 turns powers into energy fractions: 1/1.752 = 0.571, 0.501/1.752 = 0.286, 0.251/1.752 = 0.143, which sum to 1 like a discrete probability distribution. Then μ = 0 × 0.571 + 1 × 0.286 + 3 × 0.143 = 0.716 µs and σ is the standard deviation of that distribution: the same mean and variance as for a discrete random variable in Ch 1b.

  • The weights must be linear; the dB values (0, −3, −6) are negative and cannot be weights.
  • σ is not "largest delay − smallest delay": the delays span 3 µs but σ is only 1.03 µs, because late echoes carry little power.

(b)Fading type: two separate dimensions

DimensionQuestionCompareHere
Flat / frequency-selectiveDo echoes leak into the next symbol?σ with Ts: σ/Ts < 0.1 → flat1.03/20 = 0.052 → flat
Slow / fastDoes the channel change within one symbol?TC with Ts: Ts ≪ TC → slow20 µs ≪ 8.33 ms → slow

The two dimensions are independent: delay spread decides flat/selective, Doppler decides fast/slow. The symbol duration sits in between: \(\sigma_{T_m}\ll T_s\ll T_C\) means flat, slow fading. Answering only one dimension gets half marks (the lecturer said so). If σ/Ts is only slightly above 0.1 (e.g. 0.15), no conclusion can be drawn; it must be well above 0.1 to call the fading frequency-selective.

(b)Maximum Doppler shift and coherence time

Maximum Doppler shift fm: a moving receiver sees a slightly shifted frequency (like a siren rising in pitch as an ambulance approaches). The shift is \(f_d=\frac{v}{c}f_c\cos\theta\), largest when moving straight along the signal direction (cos θ = 1): \(f_m=vf_c/c\). Here v = 72 km/h ÷ 3.6 = 20 m/s and \(f_m=20\times1.8\times10^9/3\times10^8=120\) Hz.

Coherence time TC: how long the channel stays roughly constant. A larger Doppler makes the echo phases change faster, so the channel changes faster and TC is shorter: \(T_C\approx1/f_m=1/120=8.33\) ms. They are reciprocals: faster movement, larger fm, shorter TC; with no movement, fm = 0 and the channel does not change.

  • Divide km/h by 3.6 to get m/s; convert GHz to Hz; put TC and Ts in the same unit before comparing.

(c)Where ρ comes from: power ratio first, then square root

ρ = Zmin/Zrms is an amplitude ratio, but the question gives a power condition ("not more than 10 dB below its rms value"). Power is amplitude squared (P = V²/R), so amplitude is the square root of power:

\[\frac{P_{\min}}{P_{\mathrm{rms}}}=10^{-10/10}=0.1=\Big(\frac{Z_{\min}}{Z_{\mathrm{rms}}}\Big)^2=\rho^2\ \Rightarrow\ \rho=\sqrt{0.1}=0.316\]
Amplitude ratio ρPower ratio ρ²Power down by
0.7070.53 dB
0.3160.110 dB
0.10.0120 dB

For power, divide the dB value by 10; for amplitude, by 20: \(\rho=10^{-10/20}=0.316\). The ρ² in the exponent of the AFD formula is exactly the power ratio 0.1. Compare Practice 2 Q1: "signal strength is 0.1 of the rms value" refers to amplitude, so there ρ = 0.1 directly. If the question talks about amplitude (strength, envelope), use the number as ρ; if it talks about power or gives dB, convert to a power ratio and take the square root. Taking ρ = 0.1 here would give AFD = 0.33 ms, more than 3 times too small.

(c)Why divide by 20 µs

20 µs is one symbol duration: 50 k symbols/s → \(T_s=1/50000=20\) µs. A deep fade lasts 1.11 ms = 1110 µs on average, which covers 1110 / 20 ≈ 55 symbols, so one fade affects about 55 consecutive symbols. Equivalently AFD × symbol rate = 1.11×10⁻³ × 50×10³ ≈ 55.

Errors therefore come in bursts, not one at a time, which is why interleaving is used to spread consecutive bits apart before error-correcting codes fix them.

ExamCompact answer for (b) and (c)

Flat or frequency-selective: \(\sigma_{T_m}/T_s=1.03/20=0.052\lt0.1\), so the fading is flat.

Fast or slow: \(f_m=vf_c/c=120\) Hz, \(T_C\approx1/f_m=8.33\) ms ≫ \(T_s\) = 20 µs, so the channel is almost constant over a symbol and the fading is slow. Hence the signal experiences flat, slow fading.

(c) A power 10 dB below the rms value means \(\rho^2=0.1\), \(\rho=\sqrt{0.1}=0.316\). AFD \(=(e^{0.1}-1)/(\sqrt{2\pi}\times120\times0.316)=1.11\) ms. Symbols per fade \(=\text{AFD}/T_s=1.11\text{ ms}/20\ \mu s\approx55\).

In one line: power-weighted μ and σ (divide by the total power) → σ vs Ts for flat/selective, TC = 1/fm vs Ts for fast/slow → square root of the power ratio gives ρ → AFD ÷ Ts gives the symbols per fade.
◆ Key points
What it tests
μ and σ of a discrete power delay profile (Ch 2 p.30); fading type in two dimensions, flat/selective and fast/slow (p.33–36); average fade duration (p.42–43).
Background
Power-weighted: μ = ΣPiτi/ΣPi, σ = √(E[τ²] − μ²); σ/Ts < 0.1 → flat (memorise); fm = v fc/c, TC ≈ 1/fm; ρ = threshold amplitude / rms amplitude.
Method
(a) dB → linear → weighted mean and second moment from a table; (b) compare Ts with σ and with TC; (c) square root of the power ratio gives ρ → AFD → divide by Ts for the symbol count.
Common mistakes
Using dB values as weights; "10 dB down in power" means ρ = √0.1, not 0.1; answering only one dimension of the fading type gets half marks (the lecturer said so), give both flat/selective and fast/slow; convert km/h to m/s.
Check
AFD × LCR = 1 − e−ρ² = 0.095: the signal is below the threshold about 9.5% of the time.
Variations
At 200 k symbols/s, Ts = 5 µs and σ/Ts = 0.21, so the fading can no longer be called flat.

模拟 B · Q1

瑞利衰落：中断概率与衰落余量

Original question · 中英对照Mock Quiz B (self-written)

A link operates over a Rayleigh fading channel with an average received SNR of 20 dB. The receiver needs an instantaneous SNR of at least 7 dB.

某链路工作在瑞利衰落信道上，平均接收 SNR 为 20 dB。接收机要求瞬时 SNR 至少为 7 dB。

(a) State the probability density function of the instantaneous SNR γ and find the outage probability. (4 marks)

(a) 写出瞬时 SNR γ 的概率密度函数，并求中断概率。（4 分）

(b) What average SNR is needed to reduce the outage probability to 1%? Hence find the fade margin relative to the 7 dB requirement. (4 marks)

(b) 要把中断概率降到 1%，平均 SNR 需要多大？据此求相对 7 dB 要求的衰落余量。（4 分）

(c) What is the probability that the instantaneous SNR is above its average value? (2 marks)

(c) 瞬时 SNR 高于其平均值的概率是多少？（2 分）

SNR 指数分布（必背）瑞利中断 1 − e^(−γ₀/γ̄)（必背）衰落余量讲义第 2 章 p.25–26、p.51
题意讲解：这道题到底要干嘛

场景题目在讲一件什么事

链路处在瑞利衰落信道里，平均 SNR 是 20 dB，但瞬时 SNR 在不停起伏，有时会掉到 7 dB 以下，这时就中断。

题目问：中断有多频繁；要把中断压到 1%，平均 SNR 要提高到多少；以及瞬时 SNR 高于平均值的机会有多大。

符号题目里每个量是什么

γ̄ = 20 dB平均 SNR，线性值 100
γ₀ = 7 dB接收机要求的最低瞬时 SNR，线性值 5.01
p(γ)瞬时 SNR 的概率密度：瑞利衰落下是指数分布（要背）
衰落余量（fade margin）为了对付衰落，平均 SNR 要比门限多留出的 dB 数

要求每一问在问什么

  • (a) 写出 SNR 的指数分布，中断概率就是 γ 小于 γ₀ 的概率。
  • (b) 反过来：要求中断 = 1%，解出平均 SNR，再和 7 dB 相减得到衰落余量。
  • (c) 瞬时 SNR 大于平均值的概率：用指数分布的尾巴直接算。

注意读题容易误会的地方

  • 要背的是 SNR 的指数分布，不是包络的瑞利分布。
  • 代进指数前先把 dB 换成线性值。

目的这道题想让你明白什么

瑞利衰落下 SNR 大部分时间低于平均值，所以要留很大的衰落余量：中断 1% 约需 20 dB。

一句话：瑞利衰落：求 SNR 低于 7 dB 的概率，反推中断 1% 所需的平均 SNR 和衰落余量，再求 SNR 高于平均值的概率。

思路：瑞利衰落下瞬时 SNR 服从指数分布，中断概率就是它的 CDF；(b) 反过来解平均 SNR；(c) 直接用指数分布的尾巴。

  1. (a) 瑞利衰落时 SNR 服从指数分布：\(p(\gamma)=\dfrac{1}{\bar\gamma}e^{-\gamma/\bar\gamma},\ \gamma\ge0\)；中断 \(P_{out}=P(\gamma\lt\gamma_0)=1-e^{-\gamma_0/\bar\gamma}\)。
  2. 换线性：\(\bar\gamma\) = 20 dB = 100，\(\gamma_0\) = 7 dB = 5.01。
    P_out = 1 − e^(−5.01/100) = 1 − e^(−0.0501) = 0.0489
  3. (b) \(1-e^{-\gamma_0/\bar\gamma}=0.01\ \Rightarrow\ \gamma_0/\bar\gamma=\ln\frac{1}{0.99}=0.01005\ \Rightarrow\ \bar\gamma=5.01/0.01005=498.7\)，即 27.0 dB。
  4. 衰落余量 FM = 27.0 − 7 = 20.0 dB，和公式 \(10\log_{10}\frac{1}{\ln(1/(1-\varepsilon))}\approx10\log_{10}\frac1\varepsilon=20\) dB 一致。
  5. (c) \(P(\gamma\gt\bar\gamma)=e^{-\bar\gamma/\bar\gamma}=e^{-1}=0.368\)。
(a) p(γ) = (1/γ̄)e^(−γ/γ̄)，P_out ≈ 4.9%；(b) γ̄ ≈ 27 dB，衰落余量 ≈ 20 dB；(c) e⁻¹ ≈ 0.368
官方答案：无（自编模拟题）。所有数字已用 src/p2build/verify_mock.py 验算。
(c) 说明瑞利衰落下，瞬时 SNR 高于平均值的时间只有约 37%，大部分时间都低于平均值，所以要留衰落余量。没有衰落的 AWGN 信道里 SNR 一直等于平均值，平均 7 dB 就够；瑞利信道要多留 20 dB。
逐步详解：瑞利中断 1 − e−γ₀/γ̄ 从哪来（推导不考，帮助理解）

老师说瑞利分布的推导不会让推，Quiz 也不考积分；考试记住结果、会用就行。下面说明公式是怎么来的。

思路多径 → 瑞利 → 指数 → 积分

多径叠加 → I、Q 分量是高斯 → 包络 z 服从瑞利分布 → 功率 z²（以及 SNR γ）服从指数分布 → 对指数分布从 0 积到 γ₀，就是中断概率。

第 1 步为什么包络是瑞利分布

手机收到很多条相位随机的回波：\(r_I=\sum\alpha_n\cos\phi_n\)，\(r_Q=\sum\alpha_n\sin\phi_n\)。每个分量都是大量随机项之和，由中心极限定理近似为均值 0、方差 σ² 的独立高斯。包络是这个二维向量的长度 \(z=\sqrt{r_I^2+r_Q^2}\)，两个独立零均值高斯的"长度"服从瑞利分布：\(p(z)=\frac{z}{\sigma^2}e^{-z^2/(2\sigma^2)},\ z\ge0\)。

第 2 步从包络到 SNR

功率是幅度的平方：接收功率 z²，平均功率 E[z²] = 2σ²。SNR 和功率成正比：\(\gamma=z^2/N\)，平均 \(\bar\gamma=2\sigma^2/N\)。

第 3 步直接积出中断概率

"γ < γ₀"等价于"z < z₀"，\(z_0^2=\gamma_0N\)。换元 \(u=z^2/(2\sigma^2)\)、\(du=z\,dz/\sigma^2\)：

\[P_{out}=\int_0^{z_0}\frac{z}{\sigma^2}e^{-z^2/(2\sigma^2)}dz=\int_0^{z_0^2/(2\sigma^2)}e^{-u}du=1-e^{-z_0^2/(2\sigma^2)}=1-e^{-\gamma_0/\bar\gamma}\]

最后一步用了 \(z_0^2/(2\sigma^2)=\gamma_0N/(\bar\gamma N)=\gamma_0/\bar\gamma\)。

另一条路两个要背的公式是一对

对 \(1-e^{-\gamma_0/\bar\gamma}\) 关于 γ₀ 求导，就是 SNR 的指数分布 \(p(\gamma)=\frac{1}{\bar\gamma}e^{-\gamma/\bar\gamma}\)；反过来 \(\int_0^{\gamma_0}\frac{1}{\bar\gamma}e^{-\gamma/\bar\gamma}d\gamma=1-e^{-\gamma_0/\bar\gamma}\)。指数分布是 PDF，中断概率是它的 CDF，记住一个就能得到另一个。

检查合不合理

情况代入结果含义
γ₀ = 01 − e⁰0不要求 SNR，永远不中断
γ₀ → ∞1 − e−∞1永远达不到，总是中断
γ₀ = γ̄1 − e⁻¹0.63263% 的时间低于平均值（本题 (c) 的另一面）
γ₀ ≪ γ̄e−x ≈ 1 − x≈ γ₀/γ̄中断 1% 要 γ̄ ≈ 100γ₀，余量约 20 dB（本题 (b)）

用法代数时注意

  • γ₀、γ̄ 先换成线性值（7 dB → 5.01，20 dB → 100）；公式里是比值，单位一致就行。
  • 反求平均 SNR：\(\bar\gamma=\gamma_0/\ln\frac{1}{1-P_{out}}\)。
一句话：多径叠加使包络服从瑞利分布 → 功率（SNR）服从指数分布 → 指数分布从 0 积到 γ₀，得 Pout = 1 − e−γ₀/γ̄。
◆ 本题知识点
考什么
瑞利衰落下 SNR 的指数分布（必背）、瑞利中断概率（必背）、衰落余量（尽量记）（第 2 章 p.25–26、p.51）。
前置知识
p(γ) = (1/γ̄)e−γ/γ̄；P(γ < γ₀) = 1 − e−γ₀/γ̄；P(γ > x) = e−x/γ̄；ln(1/(1 − ε)) ≈ ε。
解题套路
dB 全部换成线性 → 代 1 − e−γ₀/γ̄；反求 γ̄ = γ₀ / ln(1/(1 − ε))；衰落余量 = γ̄dB − γ₀,dB。
关键一步
ε = 1% 时 ln(1/0.99) = 0.01005，所以 γ̄ ≈ 100γ₀，衰落余量 ≈ 20 dB。
易错
把 dB 值直接代进指数；要背的是 SNR 的指数分布，不是包络的瑞利 PDF；衰落余量是两个 dB 值相减。
速记
ε = 1% → 20 dB，0.5% → 23 dB，0.1% → 30 dB：中断要求每严 10 倍，余量多 10 dB。
联系
讲解页 2.3、2.7 节；2025-26 S2 Q1(a)(iii)（瑞利信道的中断容量与衰落余量）。

Approach: In Rayleigh fading the instantaneous SNR is exponentially distributed and the outage probability is its CDF; (b) solves it backwards for the average SNR; (c) uses the exponential tail directly.

  1. (a) In Rayleigh fading the SNR is exponential: \(p(\gamma)=\dfrac{1}{\bar\gamma}e^{-\gamma/\bar\gamma},\ \gamma\ge0\); outage \(P_{out}=P(\gamma\lt\gamma_0)=1-e^{-\gamma_0/\bar\gamma}\).
  2. Linear values: \(\bar\gamma\) = 20 dB = 100, \(\gamma_0\) = 7 dB = 5.01.
    Pout = 1 − e−5.01/100 = 1 − e−0.0501 = 0.0489
  3. (b) \(1-e^{-\gamma_0/\bar\gamma}=0.01\ \Rightarrow\ \gamma_0/\bar\gamma=\ln\frac{1}{0.99}=0.01005\ \Rightarrow\ \bar\gamma=5.01/0.01005=498.7\), i.e. 27.0 dB.
  4. Fade margin FM = 27.0 − 7 = 20.0 dB, matching \(10\log_{10}\frac{1}{\ln(1/(1-\varepsilon))}\approx10\log_{10}\frac1\varepsilon=20\) dB.
  5. (c) \(P(\gamma\gt\bar\gamma)=e^{-\bar\gamma/\bar\gamma}=e^{-1}=0.368\).
(a) p(γ) = (1/γ̄)e−γ/γ̄, Pout ≈ 4.9%; (b) γ̄ ≈ 27 dB, fade margin ≈ 20 dB; (c) e⁻¹ ≈ 0.368
Official answer: none (self-written mock question); all numbers checked by src/p2build/verify_mock.py
Part (c) shows that in Rayleigh fading the instantaneous SNR is above its average only about 37% of the time; most of the time it is below, which is why a fade margin is needed. In an AWGN channel without fading the SNR always equals its average, so an average of 7 dB would be enough; Rayleigh fading needs 20 dB more.
Step by step: where the Rayleigh outage 1 − e−γ₀/γ̄ comes from (not examined, for understanding)

The lecturer said the Rayleigh derivation will not be asked and the Quiz has no integration; in the exam, remember the result and use it. This only shows where it comes from.

IdeaMultipath → Rayleigh → exponential → integrate

Multipath sum → Gaussian I and Q components → Rayleigh envelope z → exponential power z² (and SNR γ) → integrating the exponential from 0 to γ₀ gives the outage probability.

Step 1Why the envelope is Rayleigh

The phone receives many echoes with random phases: \(r_I=\sum\alpha_n\cos\phi_n\), \(r_Q=\sum\alpha_n\sin\phi_n\). Each is a sum of many random terms, so by the central limit theorem they are approximately independent zero-mean Gaussians with variance σ². The envelope is the length of this 2-D vector, \(z=\sqrt{r_I^2+r_Q^2}\), and the length of two independent zero-mean Gaussians is Rayleigh: \(p(z)=\frac{z}{\sigma^2}e^{-z^2/(2\sigma^2)},\ z\ge0\).

Step 2From envelope to SNR

Power is amplitude squared: received power z², mean power E[z²] = 2σ². SNR is proportional to power: \(\gamma=z^2/N\), mean \(\bar\gamma=2\sigma^2/N\).

Step 3Integrate for the outage

"γ < γ₀" is the same as "z < z₀" with \(z_0^2=\gamma_0N\). Substitute \(u=z^2/(2\sigma^2)\), \(du=z\,dz/\sigma^2\):

\[P_{out}=\int_0^{z_0}\frac{z}{\sigma^2}e^{-z^2/(2\sigma^2)}dz=\int_0^{z_0^2/(2\sigma^2)}e^{-u}du=1-e^{-z_0^2/(2\sigma^2)}=1-e^{-\gamma_0/\bar\gamma}\]

The last step uses \(z_0^2/(2\sigma^2)=\gamma_0N/(\bar\gamma N)=\gamma_0/\bar\gamma\).

Other wayThe two formulas to memorise are a pair

Differentiating \(1-e^{-\gamma_0/\bar\gamma}\) with respect to γ₀ gives the exponential SNR distribution \(p(\gamma)=\frac{1}{\bar\gamma}e^{-\gamma/\bar\gamma}\); conversely \(\int_0^{\gamma_0}\frac{1}{\bar\gamma}e^{-\gamma/\bar\gamma}d\gamma=1-e^{-\gamma_0/\bar\gamma}\). The exponential distribution is the PDF and the outage probability is its CDF: remember one and you get the other.

CheckDoes it make sense?

CaseSubstituteResultMeaning
γ₀ = 01 − e⁰0No SNR required, never in outage
γ₀ → ∞1 − e−∞1Never reachable, always in outage
γ₀ = γ̄1 − e⁻¹0.632Below the mean 63% of the time (the other side of part (c))
γ₀ ≪ γ̄e−x ≈ 1 − x≈ γ₀/γ̄1% outage needs γ̄ ≈ 100γ₀, a margin of about 20 dB (part (b))

UseWhen substituting

  • Convert γ₀ and γ̄ to linear values first (7 dB → 5.01, 20 dB → 100); only their ratio matters, so any consistent unit works.
  • Average SNR backwards: \(\bar\gamma=\gamma_0/\ln\frac{1}{1-P_{out}}\).
In one line: the multipath sum makes the envelope Rayleigh → the power (SNR) exponential → integrating the exponential from 0 to γ₀ gives Pout = 1 − e−γ₀/γ̄.
◆ Key points
What it tests
Exponential SNR distribution in Rayleigh fading (memorise), Rayleigh outage (memorise), fade margin (try to remember) (Ch 2 p.25–26, p.51).
Background
p(γ) = (1/γ̄)e−γ/γ̄; P(γ < γ₀) = 1 − e−γ₀/γ̄; P(γ > x) = e−x/γ̄; ln(1/(1 − ε)) ≈ ε.
Method
Convert every dB value to linear → 1 − e−γ₀/γ̄; backwards γ̄ = γ₀ / ln(1/(1 − ε)); fade margin = γ̄dB − γ0,dB.
Key step
For ε = 1%, ln(1/0.99) = 0.01005, so γ̄ ≈ 100γ₀ and the fade margin ≈ 20 dB.
Common mistakes
Putting dB values into the exponent; the formula to memorise is the exponential SNR distribution, not the Rayleigh envelope PDF; the fade margin is a difference of two dB values.
Quick rule
ε = 1% → 20 dB, 0.5% → 23 dB, 0.1% → 30 dB: each 10 times stricter outage needs 10 dB more margin.
Link
Learning page sections 2.3 and 2.7; 2025-26 S2 Q1(a)(iii) (outage capacity and fade margin of a Rayleigh channel).
模拟 B · Q2

四个测量点拟合路径损耗模型，再求中断概率

Original question · 中英对照Mock Quiz B (self-written)

The path gain measurements below were taken at four distances from a transmitter. The reference distance is \(d_0=1\) m.

在离发射机四个不同距离处测得以下路径增益，参考距离 \(d_0=1\) m。

Distance d (m)
距离		\((P_r/P_t)_{dB}\)
10−68
20−86
50−91
100−105

(a) Using the simplified path loss model, find the values of \(K_{dB}\) and γ that best fit the data in the least-squares sense. (5 marks)

(a) 使用简化路径损耗模型，按最小二乘求最能拟合数据的 \(K_{dB}\) 和 γ。（5 分）

(b) Estimate the variance of the shadowing from your fit. (3 marks)

(b) 由拟合结果估计阴影的方差。（3 分）

(c) If the received power due to path loss alone is 8 dB above the minimum power required for non-outage, find the outage probability. (2 marks)

(c) 若仅由路径损耗决定的接收功率比不中断所需的最小功率高 8 dB，求中断概率。（2 分）

最小二乘拟合阴影方差 = MSEP_out = Q(余量/σ)讲义第 2 章 p.8–16
题意讲解：这道题到底要干嘛

场景题目在讲一件什么事

在离发射机 10、20、50、100 m 四个地方各测了一次路径增益。和 Practice 1 第 1 题一样：用这几个点拟合简化路径损耗模型，再用拟合的误差估计阴影，最后算中断概率。

符号题目里每个量是什么

\((P_r/P_t)_{dB}\)实测的路径增益（dB）
\(K_{dB}\)、γ要拟合的两个参数：直线的截距和斜率（斜率是 −γ）
阴影方差测量点偏离拟合直线的均方值（MSE）
8 dB余量：平均功率比门限高 8 dB

要求每一问在问什么

  • (a) 以 x = 10 log₁₀d 为横坐标，用最小二乘求最贴合 4 个点的直线，读出 KdB 和 γ。
  • (b) 逐点算"实测 − 预测"的残差，残差平方的平均就是阴影方差。
  • (c) 余量 8 dB，中断概率 = Q(8/σ)。

注意读题容易误会的地方

  • 横坐标是 10 log₁₀d，不是 log₁₀d。
  • (b) 求的是方差，(c) 代进 Q 时要用开根号后的 σ。

目的这道题想让你明白什么

实测数据 = 平均趋势（路径损耗直线）+ 随机散布（阴影）；散布的大小直接决定中断概率。

一句话：4 个测量点拟合路径损耗直线得 K、γ，残差的均方值当阴影方差，再求余量 8 dB 时的中断概率。

思路：和 Practice 1 第 1 题同一套路：dB 形式是直线 \(M=K_{dB}-\gamma x\)，\(x=10\log_{10}d\)；最小二乘求斜率和截距，残差的均方值就是阴影方差，再算 Q(余量/σ)。

  1. \(x_i=10\log_{10}d_i\) = 10、13.01、16.99、20，\(\bar x=15.00\)；\(\bar M=-87.5\)。
  2. \(x_i-\bar x\) = −5、−1.99、1.99、5；\(M_i-\bar M\) = 19.5、1.5、−3.5、−17.5。
    Σ(x − x̄)(M − M̄) = −194.95　　Σ(x − x̄)² = 57.92
  3. (a) 斜率 = −194.95 / 57.92 = −3.37，所以 γ = 3.37；\(K_{dB}=\bar M+\gamma\bar x=-87.5+3.366\times15=-37.0\) dB。
  4. (b) 预测值 \(\hat M_i=K_{dB}-\gamma x_i\) = −70.67、−80.80、−94.20、−104.33；残差 \(e_i=M_i-\hat M_i\) = +2.67、−5.20、+3.20、−0.67（加起来为 0）。
    σ²_ψ = (2.67² + 5.20² + 3.20² + 0.67²) / 4 = 44.81 / 4 = 11.2 dB²　　σ_ψ = 3.35 dB
  5. (c) \(P_{out}=Q(8/3.35)=Q(2.39)=8.4\times10^{-3}\)。
(a) γ ≈ 3.37，K_dB ≈ −37.0 dB；(b) σ²_ψ ≈ 11.2 dB²（σ ≈ 3.35 dB）；(c) P_out ≈ 0.84%
官方答案：无（自编模拟题）。所有数字已用 src/p2build/verify_mock.py 验算。
点数比 Practice 1 第 1 题少，30 分钟的 Quiz 更可能出这种规模。横坐标一定用 10 log₁₀d；直线斜率是 −γ，别丢负号；MSE 是方差，代进 Q 之前要开根号。
逐步详解：直线从哪来、最小二乘怎么推、(c) 为什么用 Q 而不是指数公式

直线M = KdB − γx 是怎么来的

模型 \(P_r/P_t=K(d_0/d)^{\gamma}\) 两边取 \(10\log_{10}\)：乘法变加法、指数变系数、\(d_0/d\) 是 \(d/d_0\) 的倒数变成负号，得 \(M=K_{dB}-\gamma\cdot10\log_{10}(d/d_0)\)。令 \(d_0=1\) m、\(x=10\log_{10}d\)，就是 \(M=K_{dB}-\gamma x\)：截距 \(K_{dB}\)、斜率 −γ 的直线。横坐标用 10 log₁₀d 是为了让斜率正好是 −γ；用 log₁₀d 斜率会变成 −10γ。

最小二乘两个偏导为 0

残差 \(e_i=M_i-(K-\gamma x_i)\)，让 \(F=\sum e_i^2\) 最小（平方：正负不会抵消、能求导、最小值就是阴影方差）。碗底两个方向斜率都为 0：

  1. \(\partial F/\partial K=0\ \Rightarrow\ \sum(M_i-K+\gamma x_i)=0\ \Rightarrow\ K=\bar M+\gamma\bar x\)：直线过平均点，残差之和为 0。
  2. \(\partial F/\partial\gamma=0\ \Rightarrow\ \sum x_i(M_i-K+\gamma x_i)=0\)，代入 K 并把 \(x_i\) 换成 \(x_i-\bar x\)：\(\gamma=-\dfrac{\sum(x_i-\bar x)(M_i-\bar M)}{\sum(x_i-\bar x)^2}\)。

本题：x̄ = 15、M̄ = −87.5，Σ(x − x̄)(M − M̄) = −194.95，Σ(x − x̄)² = 57.92，斜率 −3.37 → γ = 3.37，KdB = −37.0 dB。验证：d = 100 m 时 M = −37.0 − 3.37 × 20 = −104.3 dB，实测 −105 dB，差 −0.7 dB 就是这个点的残差（阴影）。

(c)为什么用 Q 函数，不用 1 − e−γ₀/γ̄

阴影衰落瑞利衰落
原因楼、山挡住（大尺度）多径相位抵消（小尺度）
分布dB 值是高斯（对数正态）SNR 线性值是指数分布
中断Q(余量/σ)，dB 上相减1 − e−γ₀/γ̄，线性上相除
参数余量（dB）和 σ（dB）门限 SNR、平均 SNR，没有 σ

本题的线索："path loss alone … 8 dB above" 说明平均值由路径损耗决定、阴影在它周围起伏；(b) 刚从拟合误差求出阴影的 σ = 3.35 dB。所以是阴影，用 Q：Pout = Q(8/3.35) = Q(2.39) ≈ 0.84%。

对比：如果题目换成"瑞利衰落、平均 SNR 比门限高 8 dB"，\(\gamma_0/\bar\gamma=10^{-0.8}=0.158\)，\(P_{out}=1-e^{-0.158}\approx14.7\%\)。同样 8 dB 余量，瑞利的中断高得多，因为瑞利的坑深得多。

一句话：取 dB 后模型是截距 KdB、斜率 −γ 的直线；最小二乘 = 两个偏导为 0 → 直线过平均点、斜率 = 协方差/方差；有 σ 的阴影用 Q，有平均 SNR 的瑞利用指数公式。
◆ 本题知识点
考什么
最小二乘拟合简化路径损耗模型、阴影方差 = MSE、阴影中断概率（第 2 章 p.8–16）。
前置知识
dB 形式是直线 M = KdB − γx，x = 10 log₁₀d；斜率 = Σ(x − x̄)(M − M̄)/Σ(x − x̄)²；拟合直线经过 (x̄, M̄)。
解题套路
列表：x、x − x̄、M − M̄、乘积、平方 → 斜率 → γ = −斜率 → KdB = M̄ + γx̄ → 逐点残差 → MSE → Q(余量/σ)。
关键公式
γ = −Σ(x − x̄)(M − M̄)/Σ(x − x̄)²；KdB = M̄ + γx̄；σ²ψ = (1/n)Σei²。
易错
x 只用 log₁₀d 会让 γ 大 10 倍；斜率是 −γ；MSE 除以 n（讲义的定义）；代进 Q 前要开根号。
检验
残差之和为 0（2.67 − 5.20 + 3.20 − 0.67 = 0）。
联系
Practice 1 第 1 题（6 个点的同一套路）。

Approach: Same method as Practice 1 Q1: in dB the model is a straight line \(M=K_{dB}-\gamma x\) with \(x=10\log_{10}d\). Least squares gives the slope and intercept, the mean squared residual is the shadowing variance, then Q(margin/σ).

  1. \(x_i=10\log_{10}d_i\) = 10, 13.01, 16.99, 20; \(\bar x=15.00\); \(\bar M=-87.5\).
  2. \(x_i-\bar x\) = −5, −1.99, 1.99, 5; \(M_i-\bar M\) = 19.5, 1.5, −3.5, −17.5.
    Σ(x − x̄)(M − M̄) = −194.95　　Σ(x − x̄)² = 57.92
  3. (a) Slope = −194.95 / 57.92 = −3.37, so γ = 3.37; \(K_{dB}=\bar M+\gamma\bar x=-87.5+3.366\times15=-37.0\) dB.
  4. (b) Predictions \(\hat M_i=K_{dB}-\gamma x_i\) = −70.67, −80.80, −94.20, −104.33; residuals \(e_i=M_i-\hat M_i\) = +2.67, −5.20, +3.20, −0.67 (they sum to 0).
    σ²ψ = (2.67² + 5.20² + 3.20² + 0.67²) / 4 = 44.81 / 4 = 11.2 dB²　　σψ = 3.35 dB
  5. (c) \(P_{out}=Q(8/3.35)=Q(2.39)=8.4\times10^{-3}\).
(a) γ ≈ 3.37, KdB ≈ −37.0 dB; (b) σ²ψ ≈ 11.2 dB² (σ ≈ 3.35 dB); (c) Pout ≈ 0.84%
Official answer: none (self-written mock question); all numbers checked by src/p2build/verify_mock.py
Fewer points than Practice 1 Q1, which is a more likely size for a 30-minute quiz. Always use 10 log₁₀d on the x axis; the slope is −γ, keep the sign; the MSE is a variance, take the square root before using Q.
Step by step: where the line comes from, how least squares works, why (c) uses Q and not the exponential formula

LineWhere M = KdB − γx comes from

Take \(10\log_{10}\) of \(P_r/P_t=K(d_0/d)^{\gamma}\): products become sums, the exponent becomes a factor, and \(d_0/d\) is the reciprocal of \(d/d_0\), giving a minus sign: \(M=K_{dB}-\gamma\cdot10\log_{10}(d/d_0)\). With \(d_0=1\) m and \(x=10\log_{10}d\) this is \(M=K_{dB}-\gamma x\): a line with intercept \(K_{dB}\) and slope −γ. Using 10 log₁₀d on the x axis makes the slope exactly −γ; with log₁₀d it would be −10γ.

Least squaresTwo partial derivatives equal to 0

Residual \(e_i=M_i-(K-\gamma x_i)\); minimise \(F=\sum e_i^2\) (squares: signs cannot cancel, it is differentiable, and the minimum is the shadowing variance). At the bottom of this bowl both slopes are 0:

  1. \(\partial F/\partial K=0\ \Rightarrow\ \sum(M_i-K+\gamma x_i)=0\ \Rightarrow\ K=\bar M+\gamma\bar x\): the line passes through the mean point and the residuals sum to 0.
  2. \(\partial F/\partial\gamma=0\ \Rightarrow\ \sum x_i(M_i-K+\gamma x_i)=0\); substituting K and replacing \(x_i\) by \(x_i-\bar x\): \(\gamma=-\dfrac{\sum(x_i-\bar x)(M_i-\bar M)}{\sum(x_i-\bar x)^2}\).

Here: x̄ = 15, M̄ = −87.5, Σ(x − x̄)(M − M̄) = −194.95, Σ(x − x̄)² = 57.92, slope −3.37 → γ = 3.37, KdB = −37.0 dB. Check: at d = 100 m, M = −37.0 − 3.37 × 20 = −104.3 dB against a measured −105 dB; the −0.7 dB difference is that point's residual (shadowing).

(c)Why Q and not 1 − e−γ₀/γ̄

ShadowingRayleigh fading
CauseBuildings, hills block the path (large scale)Multipath phases cancel (small scale)
DistributiondB value is Gaussian (log-normal)Linear SNR is exponential
OutageQ(margin/σ), subtract in dB1 − e−γ₀/γ̄, divide in linear
ParametersMargin (dB) and σ (dB)Threshold SNR and average SNR, no σ

Clues here: "path loss alone … 8 dB above" means the mean is set by path loss and shadowing fluctuates around it, and (b) has just found the shadowing σ = 3.35 dB from the fit. So it is shadowing and Q applies: Pout = Q(8/3.35) = Q(2.39) ≈ 0.84%.

For comparison, with "Rayleigh fading, average SNR 8 dB above the threshold": \(\gamma_0/\bar\gamma=10^{-0.8}=0.158\), \(P_{out}=1-e^{-0.158}\approx14.7\%\). The same 8 dB margin gives a much larger outage, because Rayleigh fades are much deeper.

In one line: in dB the model is a line with intercept KdB and slope −γ; least squares = both partial derivatives 0 → the line passes through the mean point and slope = covariance/variance; shadowing with σ uses Q, Rayleigh with an average SNR uses the exponential formula.
◆ Key points
What it tests
Least-squares fit of the simplified path loss model, shadowing variance = MSE, shadowing outage (Ch 2 p.8–16).
Background
In dB the model is the line M = KdB − γx with x = 10 log₁₀d; slope = Σ(x − x̄)(M − M̄)/Σ(x − x̄)²; the fitted line passes through (x̄, M̄).
Method
Table of x, x − x̄, M − M̄, products and squares → slope → γ = −slope → KdB = M̄ + γx̄ → residuals → MSE → Q(margin/σ).
Key formulas
γ = −Σ(x − x̄)(M − M̄)/Σ(x − x̄)²; KdB = M̄ + γx̄; σ²ψ = (1/n)Σei².
Common mistakes
Using log₁₀d instead of 10 log₁₀d makes γ 10 times too large; the slope is −γ; the MSE divides by n (the slides' definition); take the square root before Q.
Check
The residuals sum to 0 (2.67 − 5.20 + 3.20 − 0.67 = 0).
Link
Practice 1 Q1 (the same method with 6 points).
模拟 B · Q3

香农容量、带宽翻倍与注水

Original question · 中英对照Mock Quiz B (self-written)

A receiver gets a signal power of −90 dBm over a bandwidth of 5 MHz. The noise power spectral density is −174 dBm/Hz.

接收机在 5 MHz 带宽内收到 −90 dBm 的信号功率，噪声功率谱密度为 −174 dBm/Hz。

(a) Find the received SNR and the Shannon capacity of this AWGN channel. (3 marks)

(a) 求接收 SNR 和该 AWGN 信道的香农容量。（3 分）

(b) The bandwidth is doubled to 10 MHz while the received signal power stays the same. Find the new capacity. Does the capacity double? Explain. (3 marks)

(b) 带宽加倍到 10 MHz，接收信号功率不变。求新的容量。容量会翻倍吗？说明理由。（3 分）

(c) Two parallel subchannels, each 1 MHz wide, have SNRs of 20 and 0.5 when the whole transmit power P is given to one of them. Using water-filling, find the power allocation and the total capacity. (4 marks)

(c) 两条并行子信道，各宽 1 MHz，把全部发射功率 P 给其中一条时，SNR 分别为 20 和 0.5。用注水法求功率分配和总容量。（4 分）

香农容量（必背）噪声功率 N₀B注水法讲义第 2 章 p.48、p.54–57
题意讲解：这道题到底要干嘛

场景题目在讲一件什么事

一条 AWGN 信道：已知接收信号功率、带宽和噪声功率谱密度，先求信噪比和香农容量。

然后看两种"加资源"的方式：把带宽加倍（功率不变），容量会不会翻倍；以及有两条好坏差很多的子信道时，注水法怎么分功率。

符号题目里每个量是什么

−90 dBm接收信号功率
−174 dBm/Hz噪声功率谱密度 N₀：每赫兹的噪声功率
N = N₀B总噪声功率，在 dB 里是 −174 + 10 log₁₀B
SNR 20 和 0.5(c) 中两条子信道"全部功率都给它时"的 SNR
γ₀注水的截止值：SNR 低于它的子信道不分功率

要求每一问在问什么

  • (a) 先算噪声功率，再算 SNR（换成线性值），代香农公式。
  • (b) 带宽加倍后噪声也加倍，重新算 SNR 和容量，判断是不是翻倍并解释。
  • (c) 用注水法：先假设两条都分功率解出 γ₀，检查每条 SNR 是否高于 γ₀，不够的去掉重算，最后求容量。

注意读题容易误会的地方

  • 香农公式里 SNR 用线性值。
  • 带宽变了，噪声功率也跟着变。
  • 注水一定要检查 γj ≥ γ₀，否则会算出负功率。

目的这道题想让你明白什么

容量由带宽和 SNR 共同决定，加带宽会同时加噪声；注水法把功率给好信道，太差的子信道直接放弃。

一句话：求 AWGN 信道的 SNR 和香农容量，看带宽加倍后容量是否翻倍，再用注水法给两条子信道分功率。

思路：噪声功率 = N₀ × B，在 dB 里就是相加；SNR 换成线性值代香农公式；(c) 注水先假设所有子信道都分功率，检查有没有子信道的 SNR 低于截止值 γ₀，有就去掉重算。

  1. (a) 噪声功率：\(N=-174+10\log_{10}(5\times10^6)=-174+66.99=-107.01\) dBm。
  2. SNR = −90 − (−107.01) = 17.01 dB = 50.2。
    C = 5×10⁶ × log₂(1 + 50.2) = 5×10⁶ × 5.68 = 28.4 Mbps
  3. (b) 带宽加倍，噪声功率也加倍（多 3 dB）：N = −104.0 dBm，SNR = 14.0 dB = 25.1。
    C = 10×10⁶ × log₂(1 + 25.1) = 10×10⁶ × 4.71 = 47.1 Mbps
  4. 只增加到约 1.66 倍，没有翻倍：带宽加倍的同时 SNR 减半，log 里的部分变小了。
  5. (c) 先假设两条都分功率：\(\dfrac{2}{\gamma_0}=1+\dfrac1{20}+\dfrac1{0.5}=3.05\ \Rightarrow\ \gamma_0=0.656\)。第 2 条的 SNR 0.5 < 0.656，不该分功率，去掉重算。
  6. 只剩第 1 条：\(\dfrac1{\gamma_0}=1+\dfrac1{20}=1.05\)，\(\gamma_0=0.952\)；\(P_1/P=1/\gamma_0-1/20=1\)，全部功率给第 1 条。
    C = 1 MHz × log₂(20 / 0.952) = 1 MHz × log₂21 = 4.39 Mbps
(a) SNR ≈ 17 dB（50.2），C ≈ 28.4 Mbps；(b) C ≈ 47.1 Mbps，约 1.66 倍，不翻倍；(c) 第 2 条不分功率，P₁ = P，C ≈ 4.39 Mbps
官方答案：无（自编模拟题）。所有数字已用 src/p2build/verify_mock.py 验算。
对比：两条平分功率（各 P/2，SNR 分别为 10 和 0.25）时，C = log₂11 + log₂1.25 = 3.78 Mbps，比注水少 0.61 Mbps。把功率给太差的子信道，几乎换不来容量。
逐步详解：注水法是什么、为什么这样分

问题注水法回答什么

有几条好坏不一的并行子信道，总功率固定，怎么分功率才能让总容量最大。发射端要知道信道（informed transmitter）才能这样分。

比喻往高低不平的水池里倒水

每条子信道是水池的一格，池底高度 = 1/γj（γj 是把全部功率给它时的 SNR）：信道越好池底越低。把总功率当一桶水倒进去，水流到低处形成平的水面 1/γ₀。每格分到的功率 = 水面 − 池底：好信道多给，差信道少给，池底高过水面的一点都不给。

\[\frac{P_j}{P}=\frac{1}{\gamma_0}-\frac{1}{\gamma_j}\ (\gamma_j\ge\gamma_0),\qquad \sum_j\Big(\frac{1}{\gamma_0}-\frac{1}{\gamma_j}\Big)=1,\qquad C=\sum_{\gamma_j\ge\gamma_0}B_j\log_2\frac{\gamma_j}{\gamma_0}\]

步骤先全分，检查，不行就去掉重算

  1. 假设 n 条都分到功率：\(n/\gamma_0=1+\sum1/\gamma_j\)，解出 γ₀。
  2. 检查每条 \(\gamma_j\ge\gamma_0\)；有不满足的，去掉最差的那条（n 减 1），重新解 γ₀。
  3. 算功率和容量。

本题第 2 条被放弃

先假设都分：2/γ₀ = 1 + 1/20 + 1/0.5 = 3.05，γ₀ = 0.656；第 2 条 SNR 0.5 < 0.656（池底 1/0.5 = 2 高过水面 1/0.656 = 1.52），放弃。只剩一条：1/γ₀ = 1.05，P₁/P = 1.05 − 0.05 = 1，C = log₂(20/0.952) = log₂21 = 4.39 Mbps。平分功率只有 log₂11 + log₂1.25 = 3.78 Mbps。

对比 Practice 5 第 1 题（SNR 80、40、40）：γ₀ = 2.82，三条都大于它，全部分到功率（0.342、0.329、0.329），C ≈ 124.7 Mbps。

为什么这样分最优

容量是 log₂(1 + SNR)，增长越来越慢。差信道多给功率也抬不起 SNR，收益小；好信道每单位功率的收益高，但一直往里加也会递减。最后水面持平，就是每条信道最后一点功率的收益相等（讲义说 γ₀ 来自拉格朗日乘子，严格推导不考）。

  • 易错：γj 用线性值；一定检查 γj ≥ γ₀，否则会算出负功率；容量是 log₂(γj/γ₀)，不是 log₂(1 + γj)；去掉子信道后 n 要改。
一句话：把 1/γj 当池底、倒入总功率形成水面 1/γ₀；好的多给、差的少给、太差的不给。先全分 → 解 γ₀ → 检查 γj ≥ γ₀ → 不满足就去掉重算。
◆ 本题知识点
考什么
香农容量（必背）、噪声功率 N₀B、带宽和容量的关系、注水法（第 2 章 p.48、p.54–57）。
前置知识
C = B log₂(1 + SNR)，SNR 用线性值；N = N₀B，dB 里是 N₀ + 10 log₁₀B；注水：Σ(1/γ₀ − 1/γj) = 1，C = ΣBj log₂(γj/γ₀)。
解题套路
(a)(b) 先算噪声 → SNR（dB → 线性）→ 香农公式；(c) 假设全部分功率 → 解 γ₀ → 检查每条 γj ≥ γ₀ → 不满足就去掉最差的那条，重算。
关键一步
(c) 第一次算出 γ₀ = 0.656 > 0.5，所以第 2 条被放弃；只剩一条时 P₁ = P。
易错
SNR 用 dB 代进 log₂；带宽加倍忘了噪声也加倍；注水不检查 γj ≥ γ₀，算出负功率还照用。
直觉
带宽加倍、功率不变时 SNR 减半，容量增加不到一倍。注水法会放弃太差的子信道，把功率集中给好的。
联系
Practice 5 第 1 题（三条子信道的注水）；Practice 2 第 3 题（香农容量与 SINR）；Practice 7 第 1 题 (d)。

Approach: Noise power = N₀ × B, which in dB is a sum. Convert the SNR to linear before Shannon's formula. For (c), water-filling first assumes every subchannel gets power, then checks whether any SNR is below the cut-off γ₀; if so, drop it and recompute.
